Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that VRM significantly outperforms existing methods in capturing authentic human preferences.

</details>


### [114] [ThaiSafetyBench: Assessing Language Model Safety in Thai Cultural Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04992)
*Trapoom Ukarapol,Nut Chukamphaeng,Kunat Pipatanakul,Pakhapoom Sarapat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ThaiSafetyBench是一个针对泰语语言和文化的开源安全基准测试，包含1,954个恶意提示，用于评估大语言模型在非英语环境中的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型的安全评估主要集中于英语，缺乏对非英语语言和文化特定风险的研究。泰语作为重要的东南亚语言，其文化背景下的安全风险尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 1. 构建ThaiSafetyBench数据集，包含通用有害提示和基于泰国文化、社会、语境细微差别的针对性攻击；2. 使用该基准评估24个大语言模型，采用GPT-4.1和Gemini-2.5-Pro作为LLM-as-a-judge评估器；3. 微调基于DeBERTa的有害响应分类器ThaiSafetyClassifier以提高可复现性和成本效益。

Result: 1. 闭源模型的安全性能普遍优于开源模型；2. 泰国特定文化背景攻击的成功率始终高于通用泰语攻击，揭示了当前安全对齐方法的关键漏洞；3. ThaiSafetyClassifier达到84.4%的加权F1分数，与GPT-4.1判断结果匹配。

Conclusion: 该研究强调了在非英语语言和文化背景下进行安全评估的重要性，揭示了当前模型在文化特定攻击面前的脆弱性。通过开源基准、分类器和排行榜，为社区提供了可复现的评估工具，促进了泰语大语言模型安全性的持续改进。

Abstract: The safety evaluation of large language models (LLMs) remains largely centered on English, leaving non-English languages and culturally grounded risks underexplored. In this work, we investigate LLM safety in the context of the Thai language and culture and introduce ThaiSafetyBench, an open-source benchmark comprising 1,954 malicious prompts written in Thai. The dataset covers both general harmful prompts and attacks that are explicitly grounded in Thai cultural, social, and contextual nuances.
  Using ThaiSafetyBench, we evaluate 24 LLMs, with GPT-4.1 and Gemini-2.5-Pro serving as LLM-as-a-judge evaluators. Our results show that closed-source models generally demonstrate stronger safety performance than open-source counterparts, raising important concerns regarding the robustness of openly available models. Moreover, we observe a consistently higher Attack Success Rate (ASR) for Thai-specific, culturally contextualized attacks compared to general Thai-language attacks, highlighting a critical vulnerability in current safety alignment methods.
  To improve reproducibility and cost efficiency, we further fine-tune a DeBERTa-based harmful response classifier, which we name ThaiSafetyClassifier. The model achieves a weighted F1 score of 84.4%, matching GPT-4.1 judgments. We publicly release the fine-tuning weights and training scripts to support reproducibility. Finally, we introduce the ThaiSafetyBench leaderboard to provide continuously updated safety evaluations and encourage community participation.
  - ThaiSafetyBench HuggingFace Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/typhoon-ai/ThaiSafetyBench
  - ThaiSafetyBench Github: https://github.com/trapoom555/ThaiSafetyBench
  - ThaiSafetyClassifier HuggingFace Model: https://huggingface.co/typhoon-ai/ThaiSafetyClassifier
  - ThaiSafetyBench Leaderboard: https://huggingface.co/spaces/typhoon-ai/ThaiSafetyBench-Leaderboard

</details>


### [115] [HiFlow: Hierarchical Feedback-Driven Optimization for Constrained Long-Form Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04996)
*Yifan Zhu,Guanting Chen,Bing Wei,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HiFlow：基于分层反馈优化的约束长文本生成框架，通过双层优化和闭环反馈解决多约束下的长文本生成难题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在短文本生成上表现良好，但在复杂约束下的长文本生成仍面临挑战。这类任务涉及全局结构一致性、局部语义连贯性和约束可行性等多个紧密耦合的目标，形成困难的约束优化问题。现有方法主要依赖静态规划或离线监督，限制了生成过程中全局与局部目标的有效协调。

Method: 提出HiFlow分层反馈驱动优化框架，将生成过程建模为双层优化：规划层负责全局结构和约束建模，生成层负责条件文本生成。通过约束感知的计划筛选和双层的闭环反馈机制，实现规划质量和生成行为的联合优化，逐步引导模型产生高质量、满足约束的输出。

Result: 在多个骨干模型上的实验证实了HiFlow相对于基线方法的有效性。

Conclusion: HiFlow通过分层反馈优化框架有效解决了复杂约束下的长文本生成问题，实现了全局规划与局部生成的协同优化，显著提升了约束长文本生成的质量和可行性。

Abstract: Large language models perform well in short text generation but still struggle with long text generation, particularly under complex constraints. Such tasks involve multiple tightly coupled objectives, including global structural consistency, local semantic coherence, and constraint feasibility, forming a challenging constrained optimization problem. Existing approaches mainly rely on static planning or offline supervision, limiting effective coordination between global and local objectives during generation. To address these challenges, we propose HiFlow, a hierarchical feedback-driven optimization framework for constrained long text generation. HiFlow formulates generation as a two-level optimization process, consisting of a planning layer for global structure and constraint modeling, and a generation layer for conditioned text generation. By incorporating constraint-aware plan screening and closed-loop feedback at both levels, HiFlow enables joint optimization of planning quality and generation behavior, progressively guiding the model toward high-quality, constraint-satisfying outputs. Experiments on multiple backbones confirm HiFlow's effectiveness over baseline methods.

</details>


### [116] [NeuronMoE: Neuron-Guided Mixture-of-Experts for Efficient Multilingual LLM Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05046)
*Rongzhi Li,Hitomi Yanaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NeuronMoE：通过分析跨语言神经元多样性来指导MoE架构中专家分配的方法，在低资源语言扩展中实现约40%参数减少的同时保持性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型扩展到低资源语言具有全球可访问性意义，但为每种语言单独训练模型成本过高。现有MoE方法基于层级相似性分配专家，但语言处理在单个神经元层面表现出细粒度专业化，需要更精细的专家分配策略。

Method: 提出NeuronMoE方法，分析所有Transformer组件中的语言特定神经元，基于经验测量的跨语言神经元多样性来指导每层的专家分配。应用于Llama-3.2-3B模型，针对希腊语、土耳其语和匈牙利语等低资源语言。

Result: 在低资源语言扩展中实现约40%的平均参数减少，同时匹配LayerMoE基线的性能。发现低资源语言专家独立发展出与高资源语言相似的神经元专业化模式，这些模式集中在早期和晚期层。

Conclusion: NeuronMoE揭示了多语言模型组织语言知识的潜在通用架构原则，表明低资源语言专家会自发形成与高资源语言相似的神经元专业化模式，主要集中在模型的早期和晚期层。

Abstract: Extending large language models to low-resource languages is essential for global accessibility, but training separate models per language is prohibitively expensive. Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures address this by adding sparse language-specific parameters, but determining how many experts each layer needs remains an open question. Current approaches allocate experts based on layer-level similarity, yet language processing exhibits fine-grained specialization at individual neurons. We propose $\textbf{NeuronMoE}$, a method that analyzes language-specific neurons across all transformer components to guide expert allocation per layer based on empirically measured cross-lingual neuron diversity. Applied to Llama-3.2-3B for low-resource languages (Greek, Turkish, and Hungarian), this approach achieves approximately 40% average parameter reduction while matching the performance of the LayerMoE baseline. We find that low-resource language experts independently develop neuron specialization patterns mirroring the high-resource language, which are concentrated in early and late layers. This reveals potential universal architectural principles in how multilingual models organize linguistic knowledge.

</details>


### [117] [MUTEX: Leveraging Multilingual Transformers and Conditional Random Fields for Enhanced Urdu Toxic Span Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05057)
*Inayat Arshad,Fajar Saleem,Ijaz Hussain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MUTEX：一种结合XLM RoBERTa与CRF的多语言Transformer框架，用于乌尔都语毒性跨度检测，在token级F1得分上达到60%，是该领域的首个监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乌尔都语毒性跨度检测受限，现有系统多为句子级分类，无法识别文本内具体毒性跨度。主要挑战包括：缺乏token级标注资源、乌尔都语语言复杂性、频繁语码转换、非正式表达和丰富的形态变化。

Method: 提出MUTEX框架，结合多语言Transformer（XLM RoBERTa）与条件随机场（CRF）进行序列标注。使用手动标注的token级毒性跨度数据集，在社交媒体、在线新闻和YouTube评论等多领域数据上进行测试。

Result: MUTEX在token级F1得分上达到60%，是乌尔都语毒性跨度检测的首个监督基线。Transformer模型能更有效地隐式捕捉上下文毒性，并处理语码转换和形态变化问题。

Conclusion: MUTEX为乌尔都语毒性跨度检测提供了有效的解决方案，证明了Transformer-CRF组合在处理复杂语言现象方面的优势，为后续研究建立了基准。

Abstract: Urdu toxic span detection remains limited because most existing systems rely on sentence-level classification and fail to identify the specific toxic spans within those text. It is further exacerbated by the multiple factors i.e. lack of token-level annotated resources, linguistic complexity of Urdu, frequent code-switching, informal expressions, and rich morphological variations. In this research, we propose MUTEX: a multilingual transformer combined with conditional random fields (CRF) for Urdu toxic span detection framework that uses manually annotated token-level toxic span dataset to improve performance and interpretability. MUTEX uses XLM RoBERTa with CRF layer to perform sequence labeling and is tested on multi-domain data extracted from social media, online news, and YouTube reviews using token-level F1 to evaluate fine-grained span detection. The results indicate that MUTEX achieves 60% token-level F1 score that is the first supervised baseline for Urdu toxic span detection. Further examination reveals that transformer-based models are more effective at implicitly capturing the contextual toxicity and are able to address the issues of code-switching and morphological variation than other models.

</details>


### [118] [ARC-TGI: Human-Validated Task Generators with Reasoning Chain Templates for ARC-AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05099)
*Jens Lehmann,Syeda Khushbakht,Nikoo Salehfard,Nur A Zarin Nishat,Dhananjay Bhandiwad,Andrei Aioanei,Sahar Vahdati*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ARC-TGI是一个开源框架，通过任务家族生成器来动态生成ARC-AGI任务，解决静态数据集带来的过拟合、数据泄露和记忆化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态的ARC-AGI手工谜题集合存在过拟合、数据集泄露和记忆化问题，使得进展难以准确衡量。需要一种能够动态生成多样化任务的方法来更好地评估抽象推理能力。

Method: 开发了ARC-TGI框架，使用紧凑的Python程序作为任务家族生成器，这些生成器能够采样多样化的ARC-AGI任务同时保持潜在规则。框架采用面向求解器的表示方式，每个生成的任务都配有自然语言输入、转换推理链和部分评估的Python代码。支持任务级约束，确保训练示例共同暴露推断底层规则所需的变化。

Result: 发布了461个生成器，覆盖180个ARC-Mini任务、215个ARC-AGI-1任务（200训练，15测试）和66个ARC-AGI-2任务（55训练，11测试），实现了可扩展的数据集采样和受控基准测试。

Conclusion: ARC-TGI通过动态任务生成解决了ARC-AGI评估中的关键问题，为抽象推理能力的可扩展评估和受控基准测试提供了有效框架。

Abstract: The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC-AGI) probes few-shot abstraction and rule induction on small visual grids, but progress is difficult to measure on static collections of hand-authored puzzles due to overfitting, dataset leakage, and memorisation. We introduce ARC-TGI (ARC Task Generators Inventory), an open-source framework for task-family generators: compact Python programs that sample diverse ARC-AGI tasks while preserving a latent rule. ARC-TGI is built around a solver-facing representation: each generated task is paired with natural-language input and transformation reasoning chains and partially evaluated Python code implementing sampling, transformation, and episode construction. Crucially, ARC-TGI supports task-level constraints so that training examples collectively expose the variations needed to infer the underlying rule, a requirement for human-solvable ARC tasks that independent per-example sampling often fails to guarantee. All generators undergo human refinement and local verification to keep both grids and reasoning traces natural and consistent under variation. We release 461 generators covering 180 ARC-Mini tasks, 215 ARC-AGI-1 tasks (200 train, 15 test), and 66 ARC-AGI-2 tasks (55 train, 11 test), enabling scalable dataset sampling and controlled benchmarking.

</details>


### [119] [Representation Fidelity:Auditing Algorithmic Decisions About Humans Using Self-Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05136)
*Theresa Elstner,Martin Potthast*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"表征保真度"作为验证算法决策的新维度，通过比较算法输入表征与人类自我描述来评估决策的合理性，并基于德国信用数据集构建了首个贷款决策表征保真度基准


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前算法决策验证主要关注结果公平性和准确性，缺乏对决策依据表征质量的评估。需要一种新方法来衡量算法决策是否基于合理的人类表征，确保决策过程本身具有合理性基础

Method: 提出表征保真度概念，通过比较两种表征的距离来操作化：1) 算法决策依据的外部规定输入表征；2) 决策对象提供的自我描述（仅用于验证）。分析表征差异性质，量化差异方法，建立表征失配类型学。基于德国信用数据集构建包含30,000条合成自然语言自我描述的贷款决策基准数据集

Result: 建立了表征保真度的理论框架和操作化方法，提出了表征失配的通用类型学。创建了首个表征保真度评估基准——Loan-Granting Self-Representations Corpus 2025，包含30,000条合成自我描述及专家标注的表征失配信息

Conclusion: 表征保真度为算法决策验证提供了新维度，通过比较算法输入表征与人类自我描述，能够评估决策是否基于合理依据。提出的框架和基准为未来研究提供了基础，有助于提高算法决策的透明度和合理性

Abstract: This paper introduces a new dimension for validating algorithmic decisions about humans by measuring the fidelity of their representations. Representation Fidelity measures if decisions about a person rest on reasonable grounds. We propose to operationalize this notion by measuring the distance between two representations of the same person: (1) an externally prescribed input representation on which the decision is based, and (2) a self-description provided by the human subject of the decision, used solely to validate the input representation. We examine the nature of discrepancies between these representations, how such discrepancies can be quantified, and derive a generic typology of representation mismatches that determine the degree of representation fidelity. We further present the first benchmark for evaluating representation fidelity based on a dataset of loan-granting decisions. Our Loan-Granting Self-Representations Corpus 2025 consists of a large corpus of 30 000 synthetic natural language self-descriptions derived from corresponding representations of applicants in the German Credit Dataset, along with expert annotations of representation mismatches between each pair of representations.

</details>


### [120] [Feature Resemblance: On the Theoretical Understanding of Analogical Reasoning in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05143)
*Ruichen Xu,Wenjing Yan,Ying-Jun Angela Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过分离类比推理（基于已知相似性推断实体间共享属性），从理论上证明了Transformer中类比推理出现的三个关键机制，并通过实验验证了这些理论发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对大型语言模型推理能力的评估往往混淆多种推理类型，使得理解类比推理等特定推理能力的出现机制变得复杂。研究旨在隔离并分析类比推理在Transformer模型中的涌现机制。

Method: 通过理论分析证明三个关键结果：1）联合训练相似性和属性前提实现类比推理；2）顺序训练需要特定课程安排；3）两跳推理可简化为带恒等桥的类比推理。使用高达15亿参数的架构进行实验验证。

Result: 理论证明和实验验证表明：Transformer通过将具有相似属性的实体编码为相似表示来实现类比推理；联合训练能实现表示对齐；顺序训练需要先学习相似性结构；两跳推理需要训练数据中显式包含恒等桥。

Conclusion: Transformer通过将具有相似属性的实体编码为相似表示的统一机制实现类比推理，这种表示几何形状塑造了模型的归纳推理能力。研究揭示了类比推理在Transformer中涌现的理论基础。

Abstract: Understanding reasoning in large language models is complicated by evaluations that conflate multiple reasoning types. We isolate analogical reasoning (inferring shared properties between entities based on known similarities) and analyze its emergence in transformers. We theoretically prove three key results: (1) Joint training on similarity and attribution premises enables analogical reasoning through aligned representations; (2) Sequential training succeeds only when similarity structure is learned before specific attributes, revealing a necessary curriculum; (3) Two-hop reasoning ($a \to b, b \to c \implies a \to c$) reduces to analogical reasoning with identity bridges ($b = b$), which must appear explicitly in training data. These results reveal a unified mechanism: transformers encode entities with similar properties into similar representations, enabling property transfer through feature alignment. Experiments with architectures up to 1.5B parameters validate our theory and demonstrate how representational geometry shapes inductive reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [121] [C2-Faith: Benchmarking LLM Judges for Causal and Coverage Faithfulness in Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05167)
*Avni Mittal,Rauno Arike*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: C2-Faith基准评估LLM作为推理过程忠实性评判者的能力，关注因果性和覆盖性两个维度，发现模型表现高度依赖任务设定且存在系统性偏差


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究大语言模型是否能够可靠评估思维链推理的过程忠实性，而不仅仅是答案的合理性。当前LLM越来越多地被用作思维链推理的评判者，但其在评估推理过程忠实性方面的可靠性尚不明确。

Method: 方法包括：1) 引入C2-Faith基准，基于PRM800K构建，针对忠实性的两个互补维度：因果性（每一步是否从先前上下文逻辑推导）和覆盖性（必要的中间推理是否存在）；2) 通过受控扰动创建已知因果错误位置的示例，用非因果变体替换单个步骤；3) 使用不同删除率的受控覆盖删除；4) 评估三个前沿评判模型在三个任务上的表现：二元因果检测、因果步骤定位和覆盖性评分。

Result: 结果显示：1) 模型排名强烈依赖于任务设定，没有单一评判者在所有设置中占主导地位；2) 所有评判者在检测错误和定位错误之间存在显著差距；3) 覆盖性判断在不完整推理中系统性偏高；4) 模型表现因任务类型而异，表明LLM评判者的可靠性具有情境依赖性。

Conclusion: 结论是：这些发现阐明了LLM评判者在何时可靠以及在何处失败，为在过程级评估中选择评判者提供了实用指导。研究强调了评估推理过程忠实性需要更细致的基准和任务设定，并揭示了当前LLM评判者在过程级评估中的局限性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as judges of chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, but it remains unclear whether they can reliably assess process faithfulness rather than just answer plausibility. We introduce C2-Faith, a benchmark built from PRM800K that targets two complementary dimensions of faithfulness: causality (does each step logically follow from prior context?) and coverage (are essential intermediate inferences present?). Using controlled perturbations, we create examples with known causal error positions by replacing a single step with an acausal variant, and with controlled coverage deletions at varying deletion rates (scored against reference labels). We evaluate three frontier judges under three tasks: binary causal detection, causal step localization, and coverage scoring. The results show that model rankings depend strongly on task framing, with no single judge dominating all settings; all judges exhibit a substantial gap between detecting an error and localizing it; and coverage judgments are systematically inflated for incomplete reasoning. These findings clarify when LLM judges are dependable and where they fail, and provide practical guidance for selecting judges in process-level evaluation

</details>


### [122] [Sparse-BitNet: 1.58-bit LLMs are Naturally Friendly to Semi-Structured Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05168)
*Di Zhang,Xun Wu,Shaohan Huang,Yudong Wang,Hanyong Shao,Yingbo Hao,Zewen Chi,Li Dong,Ting Song,Yan Xia,Zhifang Sui,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Sparse-BitNet：首个将1.58位BitNet量化与动态N:M稀疏化结合的框架，在保持训练稳定的同时实现更高稀疏容忍度和1.30倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半结构化N:M稀疏化和低比特量化（如1.58位BitNet）是提升大语言模型效率的两个有前景方向，但此前研究多孤立进行。本文旨在探索二者的协同效应，特别是1.58位BitNet与N:M稀疏化的兼容性

Method: 提出Sparse-BitNet统一框架，首次联合应用1.58位量化和动态N:M稀疏化，确保训练稳定性。采用自定义稀疏张量核心实现计算加速，并在多种模型规模和训练机制（稀疏预训练和稠密到稀疏调度）下验证

Result: 1.58位BitNet在相同稀疏度下比全精度模型性能下降更小，能容忍更高的结构化稀疏度而避免精度崩溃。使用自定义稀疏张量核心，训练和推理均实现最高1.30倍加速

Conclusion: 极低比特量化与半结构化N:M稀疏化的结合是大语言模型效率优化的有前景方向，Sparse-BitNet为此提供了首个稳定训练框架和硬件加速方案

Abstract: Semi-structured N:M sparsity and low-bit quantization (e.g., 1.58-bit BitNet) are two promising approaches for improving the efficiency of large language models (LLMs), yet they have largely been studied in isolation. In this work, we investigate their interaction and show that 1.58-bit BitNet is naturally more compatible with N:M sparsity than full-precision models. To study this effect, we propose Sparse-BitNet, a unified framework that jointly applies 1.58-bit quantization and dynamic N:M sparsification while ensuring stable training for the first time. Across multiple model scales and training regimes (sparse pretraining and dense-to-sparse schedules), 1.58-bit BitNet consistently exhibits smaller performance degradation than full-precision baselines at the same sparsity levels and can tolerate higher structured sparsity before accuracy collapse. Moreover, using our custom sparse tensor core, Sparse-BitNet achieves substantial speedups in both training and inference, reaching up to 1.30X. These results highlight that combining extremely low-bit quantization with semi-structured N:M sparsity is a promising direction for efficient LLMs. Code available at https://github.com/AAzdi/Sparse-BitNet

</details>


### [123] [Guidelines for the Annotation and Visualization of Legal Argumentation Structures in Chinese Judicial Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05171)
*Kun Chen,Xianglei Liao,Kaixue Fei,Yi Xing,Xinrui Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个系统化、可操作的司法判决法律论证结构标注框架，包含命题分类、关系定义、形式化表示规则和标准化标注流程，为司法推理的大规模计算分析和法律论证挖掘提供方法论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示司法推理的逻辑组织，为计算分析提供可靠的数据基础，支持法律论证挖掘、法律推理计算建模和AI辅助法律分析等研究。

Method: 在命题层面区分四种命题类型（一般规范性命题、具体规范性命题、一般事实性命题、具体事实性命题）；在关系层面定义五种关系类型（支持、攻击、联合、匹配、同一性）；制定形式化表示规则和可视化约定；建立标准化标注工作流程和一致性控制机制。

Result: 提出了一个完整的法律论证结构标注框架，包括清晰的概念模型、形式化表示规则和实际标注程序，能够一致地图形化表示复杂的论证模式。

Conclusion: 该指南为司法推理的大规模分析以及法律论证挖掘、法律推理计算建模和AI辅助法律分析等未来研究提供了方法论支持，通过系统化的标注框架促进了法律论证结构分析的可重复性和可靠性。

Abstract: This guideline proposes a systematic and operational annotation framework for representing the structure of legal argumentation in judicial decisions. Grounded in theories of legal reasoning and argumentation, the framework aims to reveal the logical organization of judicial reasoning and to provide a reliable data foundation for computational analysis. At the proposition level, the guideline distinguishes four types of propositions: general normative propositions, specific normative propositions, general factual propositions, and specific factual propositions. At the relational level, five types of relations are defined to capture argumentative structures: support, attack, joint, match, and identity. These relations represent positive and negative argumentative connections, conjunctive reasoning structures, the correspondence between legal norms and case facts, and semantic equivalence between propositions. The guideline further specifies formal representation rules and visualization conventions for both basic and nested structures, enabling consistent graphical representation of complex argumentation patterns. In addition, it establishes a standardized annotation workflow and consistency control mechanisms to ensure reproducibility and reliability of the annotated data. By providing a clear conceptual model, formal representation rules, and practical annotation procedures, this guideline offers methodological support for large-scale analysis of judicial reasoning and for future research in legal argument mining, computational modeling of legal reasoning, and AI-assisted legal analysis.

</details>


### [124] [Transducing Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05193)
*Vésteinn Snæbjarnarson,Samuel Kiegeland,Tianyu Liu,Reda Boumasmoud,Ryan Cotterell,Tim Vieira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个通用框架，通过确定性字符串到字符串变换（特别是有限状态转换器FST）从现有语言模型派生出新的语言模型，支持概率传播和条件化，实现推理时适应特定输出格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型通常定义在字符串分布上，但下游任务需要不同的输出格式（如字节对到单词、DNA到氨基酸）。现有方法未将确定性字符串变换视为产生新的完整功能语言模型，限制了模型适应特定应用需求的能力。

Method: 提出基于确定性字符串到字符串变换的语言模型框架，重点关注有限状态转换器（FST）表示。开发算法将语言模型与FST组合，通过转换器传播概率而不改变模型参数，支持对源字符串的边际化处理和对变换输出的条件化。

Result: 实现了精确算法、高效近似和理论分析。在三个领域进行实验：将语言模型从token转换为字节、从token转换为单词、从DNA转换为氨基酸。实验证明预训练语言模型可以在推理时适应应用特定的输出要求。

Conclusion: 确定性字符串到字符串变换可以产生新的功能完整的语言模型，有限状态转换器为此提供了有效的计算框架。该方法支持推理时适应，使预训练模型能够匹配特定应用的输出格式要求，扩展了语言模型的适用性。

Abstract: Modern language models define distributions over strings, but downstream tasks often require different output formats. For instance, a model that generates byte-pair strings does not directly produce word-level predictions, and a DNA model does not directly produce amino-acid sequences. In such cases, a deterministic string-to-string transformation can convert the model's output to the desired form. This is a familiar pattern in probability theory: applying a function $f$ to a random variable $X\sim p$ yields a transformed random variable $f(X)$ with an induced distribution. While such transformations are occasionally used in language modeling, prior work does not treat them as yielding new, fully functional language models. We formalize this perspective and introduce a general framework for language models derived from deterministic string-to-string transformations. We focus on transformations representable as finite-state transducers -- a commonly used state-machine abstraction for efficient string-to-string mappings. We develop algorithms that compose a language model with an FST to *marginalize* over source strings mapping to a given target, propagating probabilities through the transducer without altering model parameters and enabling *conditioning* on transformed outputs. We present an exact algorithm, an efficient approximation, and a theoretical analysis. We conduct experiments in three domains: converting language models from tokens to bytes, from tokens to words, and from DNA to amino acids. These experiments demonstrate inference-time adaptation of pretrained language models to match application-specific output requirements.

</details>


### [125] [Diffusion LLMs can think EoS-by-EoS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05197)
*Sarah Breckner,Sebastian Schuster*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 扩散语言模型通过在答案后添加额外的EoS（序列结束）标记作为隐藏计算空间，显著提升了复杂推理任务的性能，特别是当生成长度远超过实际答案所需时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现扩散语言模型在复杂推理任务上表现优于自回归模型，特别是当生成长度设置得比实际答案所需长得多时，模型会用EoS标记填充答案。这引发了一个假设：扩散模型可能将EoS标记作为隐藏的草稿纸进行计算。

Method: 使用LLaDA1.5、LLaDA2.0-mini和Dream-v0等扩散模型，在加法、实体追踪和数独任务上进行实验。通过控制提示实验验证添加EoS标记的效果，并使用因果干预方法将EoS标记的隐藏状态替换为反事实生成的隐藏状态，观察输出变化。

Result: 实验证实添加EoS标记能提升扩散语言模型的推理能力。因果干预实验显示，将EoS标记的隐藏状态替换为反事实状态后，生成的输出经常变为反事实答案，表明EoS标记携带了解决问题的信息。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型确实能够"EoS-by-EoS"地思考，即利用EoS标记的表示作为隐藏的草稿纸进行复杂推理计算。这些看似无意义的标记实际上承载了解决问题的关键信息。

Abstract: Diffusion LLMs have been proposed as an alternative to autoregressive LLMs, excelling especially at complex reasoning tasks with interdependent sub-goals. Curiously, this is particularly true if the generation length, i.e., the number of tokens the model has to output, is set to a much higher value than is required for providing the correct answer to the task, and the model pads its answer with end-of-sequence (EoS) tokens. We hypothesize that diffusion models think EoS-by-EoS, that is, they use the representations of EoS tokens as a hidden scratchpad, which allows them to solve harder reasoning problems. We experiment with the diffusion models LLaDA1.5, LLaDA2.0-mini, and Dream-v0 on the tasks Addition, Entity Tracking, and Sudoku. In a controlled prompting experiment, we confirm that adding EoS tokens improves the LLMs' reasoning capabilities. To further verify whether they serve as space for hidden computations, we patch the hidden states of the EoS tokens with those of a counterfactual generation, which frequently changes the generated output to the counterfactual. The success of the causal intervention underscores that the EoS tokens, which one may expect to be devoid of meaning, carry information on the problem to solve. The behavioral experiments and the causal interventions indicate that diffusion LLMs can indeed think EoS-by-EoS.

</details>


### [126] [Distilling Formal Logic into Neural Spaces: A Kernel Alignment Approach for Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05198)
*Sara Candussio,Gabriele Sarti,Gaia Saveri,Luca Bortolussi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种通过蒸馏语义几何来学习形式化规范连续神经表示的框架，将符号鲁棒性核蒸馏到Transformer编码器中，实现高效可扩展的神经符号推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在局限性：符号核方法计算成本高、锚点依赖且不可逆；基于语法的神经嵌入无法捕捉底层语义结构。需要一种既能保留行为语义又计算高效的方法

Method: 采用师生设置，将符号鲁棒性核蒸馏到Transformer编码器中。使用连续的、核加权的几何对齐目标进行监督，根据语义差异比例惩罚错误。训练后编码器单次前向传播即可生成嵌入

Result: 在信号时序逻辑（STL）上验证，神经表示能忠实保留STL公式的语义相似性，准确预测鲁棒性和约束满足性，保持内在可逆性。计算成本仅为核方法的一小部分

Conclusion: 该框架实现了高效可扩展的神经符号推理和公式重构，无需在运行时重复计算核函数，为形式化规范的神经表示学习提供了新途径

Abstract: We introduce a framework for learning continuous neural representations of formal specifications by distilling the geometry of their semantics into a latent space. Existing approaches rely either on symbolic kernels -- which preserve behavioural semantics but are computationally prohibitive, anchor-dependent, and non-invertible -- or on syntax-based neural embeddings that fail to capture underlying structures. Our method bridges this gap: using a teacher-student setup, we distill a symbolic robustness kernel into a Transformer encoder. Unlike standard contrastive methods, we supervise the model with a continuous, kernel-weighted geometric alignment objective that penalizes errors in proportion to their semantic discrepancies. Once trained, the encoder produces embeddings in a single forward pass, effectively mimicking the kernel's logic at a fraction of its computational cost. We apply our framework to Signal Temporal Logic (STL), demonstrating that the resulting neural representations faithfully preserve the semantic similarity of STL formulae, accurately predict robustness and constraint satisfaction, and remain intrinsically invertible. Our proposed approach enables highly efficient, scalable neuro-symbolic reasoning and formula reconstruction without repeated kernel computation at runtime.

</details>


### [127] [Balancing Coverage and Draft Latency in Vocabulary Trimming for Faster Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05210)
*Ofir Ben Shoham*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过词汇表修剪优化推测解码中的草稿模型，在保持高覆盖率的同时显著降低延迟，提升推理吞吐量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码中草稿模型的词汇表大小存在根本性权衡：大词汇表提高token覆盖率和与目标模型的一致性，但增加延迟；小词汇表降低延迟但可能遗漏关键token。领域特定工作负载通常只使用完整词汇表的一小部分，这为词汇表修剪提供了机会。

Method: 将草稿模型词汇表选择建模为约束优化问题，平衡token覆盖率和草稿延迟。覆盖率基于训练数据中助手响应的token计算，延迟使用架构感知的FLOPs估计（捕捉语言建模头成本随词汇表大小的变化）。采用Tree-structured Parzen Estimator优化效用函数，在最小覆盖率约束下探索覆盖率-延迟Pareto前沿。

Result: 草稿词汇表最多减少97%仍保持高覆盖率。在领域特定任务上实现高达16%的延迟降低和20%的吞吐量提升，在多样化分布外任务上实现高达6.7%的吞吐量增益。

Conclusion: 词汇表修剪是优化推测解码草稿模型的有效方法，通过平衡覆盖率和延迟显著提升推理效率，特别适用于领域特定工作负载。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates inference for Large Language Models by using a lightweight draft model to propose candidate tokens that are verified in parallel by a larger target model. Prior work shows that the draft model often dominates speculative decoding latency, since it generates tokens sequentially and incurs high cost from its language modeling head as vocabulary size grows. This exposes a fundamental trade-off in draft model design: larger vocabularies improve token coverage and agreement with the target model, but incur higher draft latency, while smaller vocabularies reduce latency at the risk of missing tokens required for accurate draft generation. We address this trade-off through vocabulary trimming for draft models, motivated by the observation that domain-specific workloads use only a small fraction of the full vocabulary. We cast draft vocabulary selection as a constrained optimization problem that balances token coverage and draft latency. Coverage is computed over assistant responses in the training data, while latency is estimated using architecture-aware FLOPs that capture the cost of the language modeling head as a function of vocabulary size. We optimize a utility function with a Tree-structured Parzen Estimator to efficiently explore the coverage-latency Pareto frontier under a minimum coverage constraint. Experiments show improved speculative decoding throughput while reducing draft vocabularies by up to 97% with high coverage. On domain-specific tasks, we achieve up to 16% latency reduction and 20% throughput improvement, and up to 6.7% throughput gains on diverse out-of-distribution tasks.

</details>


### [128] [Med-V1: Small Language Models for Zero-shot and Scalable Biomedical Evidence Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05308)
*Qiao Jin,Yin Fang,Lauren He,Yifan Yang,Guangzhi Xiong,Zhizheng Wang,Nicholas Wan,Joey Chan,Donald C. Comeau,Robert Leaman,Charalampos S. Floudas,Aidong Zhang,Michael F. Chiang,Yifan Peng,Zhiyong Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Med-V1是一个30亿参数的小型语言模型，专门用于生物医学证据归因和验证任务，性能媲美前沿大模型但部署成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估文章是否支持断言对于幻觉检测和声明验证至关重要。虽然大型语言模型（LLM）有潜力自动化此任务，但达到强性能需要GPT-5等前沿模型，这些模型大规模部署成本过高。需要开发高效、准确的轻量级替代方案。

Method: 提出Med-V1系列小型语言模型（仅30亿参数），使用本研究新开发的高质量合成数据进行训练。将五个生物医学基准统一为验证格式进行评估。通过两个用例研究验证模型实用性：1）量化不同引用指令下LLM生成答案中的幻觉；2）自动识别临床实践指南中的高风险证据误归因。

Result: Med-V1在五个生物医学基准上比其基础模型性能提升27.0%至71.3%。尽管规模较小，但性能与GPT-5等前沿LLM相当，并能提供高质量的解释预测。用例研究显示：引用格式指令强烈影响引用有效性和幻觉率；GPT-5生成更多声明但幻觉率与GPT-4o相似；Med-V1能自动识别临床实践指南中的高风险证据误归因。

Conclusion: Med-V1为生物医学证据归因和验证任务提供了高效、准确的轻量级前沿LLM替代方案，适用于实际和现实世界应用。模型已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Assessing whether an article supports an assertion is essential for hallucination detection and claim verification. While large language models (LLMs) have the potential to automate this task, achieving strong performance requires frontier models such as GPT-5 that are prohibitively expensive to deploy at scale. To efficiently perform biomedical evidence attribution, we present Med-V1, a family of small language models with only three billion parameters. Trained on high-quality synthetic data newly developed in this study, Med-V1 substantially outperforms (+27.0% to +71.3%) its base models on five biomedical benchmarks unified into a verification format. Despite its smaller size, Med-V1 performs comparably to frontier LLMs such as GPT-5, along with high-quality explanations for its predictions. We use Med-V1 to conduct a first-of-its-kind use case study that quantifies hallucinations in LLM-generated answers under different citation instructions. Results show that the format instruction strongly affects citation validity and hallucination, with GPT-5 generating more claims but exhibiting hallucination rates similar to GPT-4o. Additionally, we present a second use case showing that Med-V1 can automatically identify high-stakes evidence misattributions in clinical practice guidelines, revealing potentially negative public health impacts that are otherwise challenging to identify at scale. Overall, Med-V1 provides an efficient and accurate lightweight alternative to frontier LLMs for practical and real-world applications in biomedical evidence attribution and verification tasks. Med-V1 is available at https://github.com/ncbi-nlp/Med-V1.

</details>


### [129] [A theoretical model of dynamical grammatical gender shifting based on set-valued set function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03510)
*Mohamed El Idrissi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于模板的模块化认知模型，用于解释名词在语义和形态句法特征上的变异，特别是语法性别转换现象，并通过形式化数学框架统一理解跨语言的形态标记复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索名词的多样性特征，包括语义（可数/不可数）和形态句法（阳性/阴性）区别，特别关注语法性别转换这一跨语言普遍现象，旨在通过形式模型揭示词位变异的潜在模式。

Method: 提出了一种新的计算组件，专门用于将词汇项与形态模板配对，建立了基于模板的模块化认知模型。该模型通过集合值函数 h: P(M) → P(M) 形式化表示，预测词汇项到形态模板的非线性动态映射。通过将词汇项（如 funas）与包含语法特征（如 +SG, -PL, -M, +F 等）的模板配对，并生成拼写形式（如 ða-funast 'cow'）。

Result: 通过经验观察，展示了这些转换（包括非性别转换）在词汇变化过程中如何产生，特别是在Riffian语言中。模型表明这些变异标记源于词汇和意义形成过程中的模板转换。通过形式化证明转换适用于名词到名词的派生，挑战并拓宽了传统的构词观点。

Conclusion: 该数学模型不仅有助于更深入理解形态句法变异，还为需要精确建模语言模式的其他领域提供了潜在应用。通过统一框架解释了跨语言形态标记的复杂性，为理解语法性别转换等语言现象提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: This study investigates the diverse characteristics of nouns, focusing on both semantic (e.g., countable/uncountable) and morphosyntactic (e.g., masculine/feminine) distinctions. We explore inter-word variations for gender markers in noun morphology. Grammatical gender shift is a widespread phenomenon in languages around the world. The aim is to uncover through a formal model the underlying patterns governing the variation of lexemes. To this end, we propose a new computational component dedicated to pairing items with morphological templates (e.g., the result of a generated item-template pair: (funas, $\{N, +SG, -PL, -M, +F, -COL, +SING\}$), with its spell-out form: $ð$a-funast 'cow'). This process is formally represented by the Template-Based and Modular Cognitive model. This proposed model, defined by a set-valued set function $h : \mathscr{P}(M) \rightarrow \mathscr{P}(M)$, predicts the nonlinear dynamic mapping of lexical items onto morphological templates. By applying this formalism, we present a unified framework for understanding the complexities of morphological markings across languages. Through empirical observations, we demonstrate how these shifts, as well as non-gender shifts, arise during lexical changes, especially in Riffian. Our model posits that these variant markings emerge due to template shifts occurring during word and meaning's formation. By formally demonstrating that conversion is applicable to noun-to-noun derivation, we challenge and broaden the conventional view of word formation. This mathematical model not only contributes to a deeper understanding of morphosyntactic variation but also offers potential applications in other fields requiring precise modelling of linguistic patterns.

</details>


### [130] [PersianPunc: A Large-Scale Dataset and BERT-Based Approach for Persian Punctuation Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05314)
*Mohammad Javad Ranjbar Kalahroodi,Heshaam Faili,Azadeh Shakery*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了PersianPunc，一个用于波斯语标点恢复的大规模高质量数据集，并基于ParsBERT构建了高效的标点恢复模型，解决了波斯语标点恢复研究不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标点恢复对于提高自动语音识别输出的可读性和下游应用至关重要，但波斯语标点恢复研究仍然不足，缺乏高质量数据集和高效模型。

Method: 通过系统聚合和过滤现有文本资源构建了包含1700万样本的PersianPunc数据集；将标点恢复定义为token级序列标注任务，并微调ParsBERT模型。

Result: BERT方法在测试集上达到91.33%的宏平均F1分数，且计算效率高适合实时应用；相比大语言模型，BERT方法避免了过度修正问题，计算需求更低。

Conclusion: 提出的轻量级BERT方法在波斯语标点恢复上表现优异，公开的数据集和模型将促进波斯语NLP研究，其框架可扩展到其他形态丰富、资源匮乏的语言。

Abstract: Punctuation restoration is essential for improving the readability and downstream utility of automatic speech recognition (ASR) outputs, yet remains underexplored for Persian despite its importance. We introduce PersianPunc, a large-scale, high-quality dataset of 17 million samples for Persian punctuation restoration, constructed through systematic aggregation and filtering of existing textual resources. We formulate punctuation restoration as a token-level sequence labeling task and fine-tune ParsBERT to achieve strong performance. Through comparative evaluation, we demonstrate that while large language models can perform punctuation restoration, they suffer from critical limitations: over-correction tendencies that introduce undesired edits beyond punctuation insertion (particularly problematic for speech-to-text pipelines) and substantially higher computational requirements. Our lightweight BERT-based approach achieves a macro-averaged F1 score of 91.33% on our test set while maintaining efficiency suitable for real-time applications. We make our dataset (https://huggingface.co/datasets/MohammadJRanjbar/persian-punctuation-restoration) and model (https://huggingface.co/MohammadJRanjbar/parsbert-persian-punctuation) publicly available to facilitate future research in Persian NLP and provide a scalable framework applicable to other morphologically rich, low-resource languages.

</details>


### [131] [A Multilingual Human Annotated Corpus of Original and Easy-to-Read Texts to Support Access to Democratic Participatory Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05345)
*Stefan Bott,Verena Riegler,Horacio Saggion,Almudena Rascón Alcaina,Nouran Khallaf*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究为西班牙语、加泰罗尼亚语和意大利语创建了一个高质量的人工简化文本语料库，旨在支持自动文本简化研究并促进民主参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动文本简化研究常受限于高质量训练和评估材料的可用性，这对英语如此，对西班牙语、加泰罗尼亚语和意大利语等资源较少的语言更是如此。理解信息是自主生活和社会参与的关键因素，对民主进程参与尤为重要。

Method: 在iDEM项目中开发，收集与民主参与主题相关的原始文本，基于相关性、版权可用性和道德标准选择不同文本类型。所有文本均由文本简化专家人工简化至"易于阅读"水平。

Result: 创建了包含西班牙语、加泰罗尼亚语和意大利语的高质量人工简化文本语料库，其中加泰罗尼亚语语料库是该类别的首个标注语料库。该语料库对西班牙语和意大利语也是重要贡献，提供了这些领域中罕见的高质量人工标注语言资源。

Conclusion: 该语料库填补了资源较少语言在自动文本简化研究中的空白，将免费向公众开放，支持相关研究并促进民主参与。

Abstract: Being able to understand information is a key factor for a self-determined life and society. It is also very important for participating in democratic processes. The study of automatic text simplification is often limited by the availability of high quality material for the training and evaluation on automatic simplifiers. This is true for English, but more so for less resourced languages like Spanish, Catalan and Italian. In order to fill this gap, we present a corpus of original texts for these 3 languages, with high quality simplification produced by human experts in text simplification. It was developed within the iDEM project to assess the impact of Easy-to-Read (E2R) language for democratic participation. The original texts were compiled from domains related to this topic. The corpus includes different text types, selected based on relevance, copyright availability, and ethical standards. All texts were simplified to E2R level. The corpus is particularity valuable because it includes the first annotated corpus of its kind for the Catalan language. It also represents a noteworthy contribution for Spanish and Italian, offering high-quality, human-annotated language resources that are rarely available in these domains. The corpus will be made freely accessible to the public.

</details>


### [132] [Exploring the potential and limitations of Model Merging for Multi-Domain Adaptation in ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05354)
*Carlos Carvalho,Francisco Teixeira,Thomas Rolland,Alberto Abad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了11种模型合并算法在10个欧洲葡萄牙语ASR领域的表现，并提出了一种改进的BoostedTSV-M算法，在保持分布外泛化能力的同时优于全微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语音基础模型通常通过领域特定微调进行适配，产生多个定制化检查点。当新数据可用时，重复全微调计算成本过高，因此模型合并成为多任务训练的可扩展替代方案。

Method: 研究评估了11种模型合并算法在10个欧洲葡萄牙语领域的ASR任务，并提出BoostedTSV-M算法——基于TSV-M改进，通过奇异值提升缓解秩崩溃问题并提高数值稳定性。

Result: 整体方法在欧洲葡萄牙语上优于全微调，同时在单一模型中保持了分布外泛化能力。BoostedTSV-M算法在领域内准确性、分布偏移鲁棒性以及英语和多语言性能方面表现良好。

Conclusion: 模型合并是多领域ASR的有效可扩展解决方案，提出的BoostedTSV-M算法通过缓解秩崩溃和提升数值稳定性，在保持泛化能力的同时实现了优于全微调的性能。

Abstract: Model merging is a scalable alternative to multi-task training that combines the capabilities of multiple specialised models into a single model. This is particularly attractive for large speech foundation models, which are typically adapted through domain-specific fine-tuning, resulting in multiple customised checkpoints, for which repeating full fine-tuning when new data becomes available is computationally prohibitive. In this work, we study model merging for multi-domain ASR and benchmark 11 merging algorithms for 10 European Portuguese domains, evaluating in-domain accuracy, robustness under distribution shift, as well as English and multilingual performance. We further propose BoostedTSV-M, a new merging algorithm based on TSV-M that mitigates rank collapse via singular-value boosting and improves numerical stability. Overall, our approach outperforms full fine-tuning on European Portuguese while preserving out-of-distribution generalisation in a single model.

</details>


### [133] [DiSCTT: Consensus-Guided Self-Curriculum for Efficient Test-Time Adaptation in Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05357)
*Mohammad Mahdi Moradi,Sudhir Mudur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DiSCTT：基于难度感知和共识引导的自课程框架，根据推理轨迹的一致性动态分配测试时优化策略，提高LLM推理性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时适应方法对所有输入采用统一的优化目标，导致在异构推理问题上效率低下或不稳定，需要根据实例难度和不确定性进行差异化处理

Method: 提出DiSCTT框架：1）从采样推理轨迹的一致性估计实例级认知不确定性；2）高一致性输入使用多数同意解作为伪标签进行监督微调；3）低一致性输入使用共识正则化目标的强化学习，在相关性约束下鼓励多样性

Result: 在广泛的数学和通用推理基准测试中，DiSCTT持续优于强基线，实现更高准确率、更低方差，并显著减少计算和训练时间

Conclusion: 显式考虑实例难度和不确定性能够实现更稳定、高效和有效的推理模型测试时适应

Abstract: Test-time adaptation offers a promising avenue for improving reasoning performance in large language models without additional supervision, but existing approaches often apply a uniform optimization objective across all inputs, leading to inefficient or unstable adaptation on heterogeneous reasoning problems. We propose DiSCTT, a difficulty-aware, consensus-guided self-curriculum framework that dynamically allocates test-time optimization strategies based on instance-level epistemic uncertainty estimated from agreement among sampled reasoning trajectories. Inputs with high consensus are consolidated via supervised fine-tuning using majority-agreed solutions as pseudo-labels, while low-consensus inputs are optimized via reinforcement learning with a consensus-regularized objective that encourages diversity under relevance constraints. Across a broad suite of mathematical and general reasoning benchmarks, DiSCTT consistently outperforms strong test-time adaptation baselines, achieving higher accuracy with reduced variance and substantially lower computation and wall-clock training times. These results demonstrate that explicitly accounting for instance difficulty and uncertainty enables more stable, efficient, and effective test-time adaptation for reasoning models.

</details>


### [134] [Progressive Residual Warmup for Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05369)
*Tianhao Chen,Xin Xu,Lu Yin,Hao Chen,Yang Wang,Shizhe Diao,Can Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ProRes是一种用于Transformer语言模型预训练的新方法，通过渐进式残差预热实现"早期层先学习"的哲学，深层等待浅层稳定后再参与学习，从而提高训练稳定性、收敛速度和下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer架构作为大多数现代大语言模型的基础，其预训练稳定性和收敛速度至关重要。由于序列堆叠层之间存在逻辑依赖关系，需要一种方法来协调不同层的训练进度。

Method: 提出渐进式残差预热（ProRes）方法：将每层的残差乘以一个从0逐渐增加到1的标量，深层需要更长的预热步数。这样深层会等待早期层进入更稳定的状态后再参与学习，实现"早期层先学习"的训练策略。

Result: 在不同模型规模、归一化和初始化方案下的预训练实验表明，ProRes不仅稳定了预训练过程，还引入了独特的优化轨迹，实现了更快的收敛速度、更强的泛化能力和更好的下游任务性能。

Conclusion: ProRes是一种有效的Transformer语言模型预训练方法，通过协调不同层的训练进度，显著提高了训练稳定性、收敛速度和模型性能，为大规模语言模型预训练提供了新的优化策略。

Abstract: Transformer architectures serve as the backbone for most modern Large Language Models, therefore their pretraining stability and convergence speed are of central concern. Motivated by the logical dependency of sequentially stacked layers, we propose Progressive Residual Warmup (ProRes) for language model pretraining. ProRes implements an "early layer learns first" philosophy by multiplying each layer's residual with a scalar that gradually warms up from 0 to 1, with deeper layers taking longer warmup steps. In this way, deeper layers wait for early layers to settle into a more stable regime before contributing to learning. We demonstrate the effectiveness of ProRes through pretraining experiments across various model scales, as well as normalization and initialization schemes. Comprehensive analysis shows that ProRes not only stabilizes pretraining but also introduces a unique optimization trajectory, leading to faster convergence, stronger generalization and better downstream performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/dandingsky/ProRes.

</details>


### [135] [An Exploration-Analysis-Disambiguation Reasoning Framework for Word Sense Disambiguation with Low-Parameter LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05400)
*Deshan Sumanathilaka,Nicholas Micallef,Julian Hough*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 低参数LLM通过推理驱动的微调策略在词义消歧任务上达到与GPT-4-Turbo相当的性能，显著降低计算和能耗需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词义消歧（WSD）是NLP的关键挑战，特别是处理罕见或领域特定词义时。虽然大参数LLM（如GPT-4-Turbo）在WSD上表现优异，但其计算和能耗需求限制了可扩展性。本研究探索低参数LLM（<4B参数）是否通过推理驱动的微调策略能达到可比性能。

Method: 使用增强版FEWS数据集（包含半自动、富含推理的标注），对8个小规模开源LLM（如Gemma和Qwen）进行微调。采用思维链推理与邻近词分析相结合的方法，强调推理驱动的词义识别。

Result: Gemma-3-4B和Qwen-3-4B模型在FEWS数据集上持续超越所有中等参数基线和SOTA模型，并在未见词义上表现出鲁棒泛化能力。在"Fool Me If You Can"数据集上的评估证实了无需任务特定微调的强大跨域适应性。推理驱动微调的低参数LLM在零样本设置下达到与GPT-4-Turbo相当的性能。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的推理中心微调策略，低参数LLM能够提供准确的词义消歧，同时显著降低计算和能耗需求，为资源受限环境下的WSD应用提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Word Sense Disambiguation (WSD) remains a key challenge in Natural Language Processing (NLP), especially when dealing with rare or domain-specific senses that are often misinterpreted. While modern high-parameter Large Language Models (LLMs) such as GPT-4-Turbo have shown state-of-the-art WSD performance, their computational and energy demands limit scalability. This study investigates whether low-parameter LLMs (<4B parameters) can achieve comparable results through fine-tuning strategies that emphasize reasoning-driven sense identification. Using the FEWS dataset augmented with semi-automated, rationale-rich annotations, we fine-tune eight small-scale open-source LLMs (e.g. Gemma and Qwen). Our results reveal that Chain-of-Thought (CoT)-based reasoning combined with neighbour-word analysis achieves performance comparable to GPT-4-Turbo in zero-shot settings. Importantly, Gemma-3-4B and Qwen-3-4B models consistently outperform all medium-parameter baselines and state-of-the-art models on FEWS, with robust generalization to unseen senses. Furthermore, evaluation on the unseen "Fool Me If You Can'' dataset confirms strong cross-domain adaptability without task-specific fine-tuning. This work demonstrates that with carefully crafted reasoning-centric fine-tuning, low-parameter LLMs can deliver accurate WSD while substantially reducing computational and energy demands.

</details>


### [136] [FlashAttention-4: Algorithm and Kernel Pipelining Co-Design for Asymmetric Hardware Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05451)
*Ted Zadouri,Markus Hoehnerbach,Jay Shah,Timmy Liu,Vijay Thakkar,Tri Dao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FlashAttention-4针对Blackwell GPU架构优化注意力计算，通过异步MMA操作、软件模拟指数计算和利用张量内存等技术，相比cuDNN和Triton实现显著加速，并在CuTe-DSL中实现快速编译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI行业快速转向Blackwell架构（B200/GB200），硬件特性发生根本变化：张量核心吞吐量翻倍，但共享内存带宽、指数单元等资源扩展较慢或保持不变，导致FlashAttention-3等现有优化方案无法充分发挥新硬件性能，需要针对Blackwell的不对称硬件缩放特性重新设计注意力优化方案。

Method: 1. 重新设计流水线，利用完全异步MMA操作和更大的tile尺寸；2. 软件模拟指数计算和条件softmax重缩放，减少非矩阵乘法操作；3. 利用张量内存和2-CTA MMA模式减少反向传播中的共享内存流量和原子加法操作。此外，完全在CuTe-DSL（嵌入Python）中实现，相比传统C++模板方法显著加快编译速度。

Result: 在B200 GPU上使用BF16精度，FlashAttention-4相比cuDNN 9.13实现最高1.3倍加速，相比Triton实现最高2.7倍加速，达到1613 TFLOPs/s（71%利用率）。编译时间比传统C++模板方法快20-30倍。

Conclusion: 针对Blackwell GPU的不对称硬件缩放特性，FlashAttention-4通过算法创新和CuTe-DSL实现，显著提升了注意力计算的性能，为大规模语言模型和长上下文应用提供了高效的注意力层实现方案。

Abstract: Attention, as a core layer of the ubiquitous Transformer architecture, is the bottleneck for large language models and long-context applications. While FlashAttention-3 optimized attention for Hopper GPUs through asynchronous execution and warp specialization, it primarily targets the H100 architecture. The AI industry has rapidly transitioned to deploying Blackwell-based systems such as the B200 and GB200, which exhibit fundamentally different performance characteristics due to asymmetric hardware scaling: tensor core throughput doubles while other functional units (shared memory bandwidth, exponential units) scale more slowly or remain unchanged. We develop several techniques to address these shifting bottlenecks on Blackwell GPUs: (1) redesigned pipelines that exploit fully asynchronous MMA operations and larger tile sizes, (2) software-emulated exponential and conditional softmax rescaling that reduces non-matmul operations, and (3) leveraging tensor memory and the 2-CTA MMA mode to reduce shared memory traffic and atomic adds in the backward pass. We demonstrate that our method, FlashAttention-4, achieves up to 1.3$\times$ speedup over cuDNN 9.13 and 2.7$\times$ over Triton on B200 GPUs with BF16, reaching up to 1613 TFLOPs/s (71% utilization). Beyond algorithmic innovations, we implement FlashAttention-4 entirely in CuTe-DSL embedded in Python, achieving 20-30$\times$ faster compile times compared to traditional C++ template-based approaches while maintaining full expressivity.

</details>


### [137] [DEBISS: a Corpus of Individual, Semi-structured and Spoken Debates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05459)
*Klaywert Danillo Ferreira de Souza,David Eduardo Pereira,Cláudio E. C. Campelo,Larissa Lucena Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DEBISS语料库：一个包含口语和个体辩论的半结构化辩论语料库，具有多种NLP任务标注


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辩论在日常生活中有广泛应用，但由于辩论形式多样、结构复杂，现有辩论语料库稀缺，难以满足研究需求

Method: 构建DEBISS语料库，包含口语和个体辩论，具有半结构化特征，并标注了多种NLP任务

Result: 创建了一个具有广泛NLP任务标注的辩论语料库，包括语音转文本、说话人分离、论点挖掘和辩手质量评估等

Conclusion: DEBISS语料库填补了辩论研究领域语料库稀缺的空白，为多种NLP任务提供了有价值的资源

Abstract: The process of debating is essential in our daily lives, whether in studying, work activities, simple everyday discussions, political debates on TV, or online discussions on social networks. The range of uses for debates is broad. Due to the diverse applications, structures, and formats of debates, developing corpora that account for these variations can be challenging, and the scarcity of debate corpora in the state of the art is notable. For this reason, the current research proposes the DEBISS corpus: a collection of spoken and individual debates with semi-structured features. With a broad range of NLP task annotations, such as speech-to-text, speaker diarization, argument mining, and debater quality assessment.

</details>


### [138] [NCTB-QA: A Large-Scale Bangla Educational Question Answering Dataset and Benchmarking Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05462)
*Abrar Eyasir,Tahsin Ahmed,Muhammad Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了NCTB-QA，一个包含87,805个问答对的大规模孟加拉语阅读理解数据集，专门针对不可回答问题设计，用于提升低资源语言问答系统的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言的阅读理解系统在处理不可回答问题（即正确答案不在上下文中的问题）时表现不佳，容易产生不可靠的回答。现有孟加拉语数据集缺乏平衡的不可回答问题分布，无法有效评估系统在这方面的能力。

Method: 从孟加拉国国家课程与教科书委员会出版的50本教科书中提取87,805个问答对，构建NCTB-QA数据集。该数据集保持可回答问题（57.25%）和不可回答问题（42.75%）的平衡分布，并包含对抗性设计的实例（包含合理的干扰项）。使用三种基于Transformer的模型（BERT、RoBERTa、ELECTRA）进行基准测试，并通过微调评估性能改进。

Result: 微调后模型性能显著提升：BERT的F1分数相对提高313%（从0.150到0.620）。所有模型的BERTScore（语义答案质量）也显著提高。研究结果表明，在低资源环境下，领域特定的微调对于获得鲁棒性能至关重要。

Conclusion: NCTB-QA为孟加拉语教育问答系统提供了一个具有挑战性的基准测试，证明了平衡的不可回答问题分布和对抗性设计对于评估系统鲁棒性的重要性。该研究强调了领域特定微调在低资源语言处理中的关键作用。

Abstract: Reading comprehension systems for low-resource languages face significant challenges in handling unanswerable questions. These systems tend to produce unreliable responses when correct answers are absent from context. To solve this problem, we introduce NCTB-QA, a large-scale Bangla question answering dataset comprising 87,805 question-answer pairs extracted from 50 textbooks published by Bangladesh's National Curriculum and Textbook Board. Unlike existing Bangla datasets, NCTB-QA maintains a balanced distribution of answerable (57.25%) and unanswerable (42.75%) questions. NCTB-QA also includes adversarially designed instances containing plausible distractors. We benchmark three transformer-based models (BERT, RoBERTa, ELECTRA) and demonstrate substantial improvements through fine-tuning. BERT achieves 313% relative improvement in F1 score (0.150 to 0.620). Semantic answer quality measured by BERTScore also increases significantly across all models. Our results establish NCTB-QA as a challenging benchmark for Bangla educational question answering. This study demonstrates that domain-specific fine-tuning is critical for robust performance in low-resource settings.

</details>


### [139] [Leveraging LLM Parametric Knowledge for Fact Checking without Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05471)
*Artem Vazhentsev,Maria Marina,Daniil Moskovskiy,Sergey Pletenev,Mikhail Seleznyov,Mikhail Salnikov,Elena Tutubalina,Vasily Konovalov,Irina Nikishina,Alexander Panchenko,Viktor Moskvoretskii*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出无需检索的事实核查任务，通过内部表征交互方法INTRA实现SOTA性能，验证了不依赖外部检索的事实核查可行性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于检索的事实核查方法受限于检索错误和外部数据可用性，未能充分利用LLM内在的事实核查能力，需要探索不依赖外部检索的事实核查方法

Method: 提出无需检索的事实核查任务，引入综合评估框架测试泛化能力，开发INTRA方法利用内部表征交互实现事实核查

Result: 在9个数据集、18种方法和3个模型上的实验表明，基于logit的方法通常表现不佳，而INTRA方法利用内部表征交互实现了SOTA性能，具有强泛化能力

Conclusion: 无需检索的事实核查是一个有前景的研究方向，可以补充基于检索的框架，提高可扩展性，并可作为训练奖励信号或生成过程的组成部分

Abstract: Trustworthiness is a core research challenge for agentic AI systems built on Large Language Models (LLMs). To enhance trust, natural language claims from diverse sources, including human-written text, web content, and model outputs, are commonly checked for factuality by retrieving external knowledge and using an LLM to verify the faithfulness of claims to the retrieved evidence. As a result, such methods are constrained by retrieval errors and external data availability, while leaving the models intrinsic fact-verification capabilities largely unused. We propose the task of fact-checking without retrieval, focusing on the verification of arbitrary natural language claims, independent of their source. To study this setting, we introduce a comprehensive evaluation framework focused on generalization, testing robustness to (i) long-tail knowledge, (ii) variation in claim sources, (iii) multilinguality, and (iv) long-form generation. Across 9 datasets, 18 methods and 3 models, our experiments indicate that logit-based approaches often underperform compared to those that leverage internal model representations. Building on this finding, we introduce INTRA, a method that exploits interactions between internal representations and achieves state-of-the-art performance with strong generalization. More broadly, our work establishes fact-checking without retrieval as a promising research direction that can complement retrieval-based frameworks, improve scalability, and enable the use of such systems as reward signals during training or as components integrated into the generation process.

</details>


### [140] [Reasoning Theater: Disentangling Model Beliefs from Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05488)
*Siddharth Boppana,Annabel Ma,Max Loeffler,Raphael Sarfati,Eric Bigelow,Atticus Geiger,Owen Lewis,Jack Merullo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型存在"表演性思维链"现象：模型在早期已确定最终答案，但仍继续生成推理过程而不暴露其内部信念。通过激活探针、早期强制回答和思维链监控等方法，揭示了任务难度相关的差异，并提出探针引导的早期退出可大幅减少计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索大型语言模型在思维链推理中的真实认知过程，区分"表演性推理"（模型已确定答案但仍生成推理过程）与"真实推理"（模型在生成过程中逐步形成答案）。旨在理解模型内部信念与外部生成行为之间的不一致性。

Method: 采用三种方法对比分析：1) 激活探针：从模型内部激活中解码最终答案；2) 早期强制回答：在思维链中途强制模型给出答案；3) 思维链监控：监控推理过程。在两个大模型（DeepSeek-R1 671B和GPT-OSS 120B）上测试，使用MMLU（简单回忆任务）和GPQA-Diamond（困难多跳推理任务）数据集。

Result: 1) 对于简单MMLU任务，模型在思维链早期就能从激活中解码出最终答案，远早于监控器能判断的时间；2) 对于困难GPQA-Diamond任务，模型表现出真实推理过程；3) 转折点（如回溯、"顿悟"时刻）主要出现在探针显示信念大幅变化时；4) 探针引导的早期退出在MMLU上减少80%的token，在GPQA-Diamond上减少30%的token，同时保持相似准确率。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型存在表演性思维链现象，特别是在简单任务中。激活探针是检测表演性推理的有效工具，并能实现自适应计算，通过早期退出大幅减少计算开销而不损失准确率。模型的行为转折点与内部信念变化相关，表明这些行为反映了真实的不确定性而非纯粹的"推理表演"。

Abstract: We provide evidence of performative chain-of-thought (CoT) in reasoning models, where a model becomes strongly confident in its final answer, but continues generating tokens without revealing its internal belief. Our analysis compares activation probing, early forced answering, and a CoT monitor across two large models (DeepSeek-R1 671B & GPT-OSS 120B) and find task difficulty-specific differences: The model's final answer is decodable from activations far earlier in CoT than a monitor is able to say, especially for easy recall-based MMLU questions. We contrast this with genuine reasoning in difficult multihop GPQA-Diamond questions. Despite this, inflection points (e.g., backtracking, 'aha' moments) occur almost exclusively in responses where probes show large belief shifts, suggesting these behaviors track genuine uncertainty rather than learned "reasoning theater." Finally, probe-guided early exit reduces tokens by up to 80% on MMLU and 30% on GPQA-Diamond with similar accuracy, positioning attention probing as an efficient tool for detecting performative reasoning and enabling adaptive computation.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [How Professional Visual Artists are Negotiating Generative AI in the Workplace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04537)
*Harry H. Jiang,Jordan Taylor,William Agnew*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 对378名专业视觉艺术家的调查显示，大多数艺术家强烈反对使用生成式AI，通过拒绝策略应对职场压力，并报告AI对其工作场所产生负面影响的普遍现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式AI在流行媒体和HCI研究中受到艺术家的广泛批评，但对其如何影响专业艺术家的工作场所和职业生涯仍缺乏深入理解。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探究生成式AI对专业视觉艺术家职业和工作环境的实际影响。

Method: 通过对378名经过验证的专业视觉艺术家进行问卷调查，研究他们关于生成式AI如何影响其职业生涯和工作场所的看法与经验。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 大多数视觉艺术家强烈反对使用生成式AI（无论是文本还是视觉），并通过各种拒绝策略来应对职场中的AI使用压力；(2) 艺术家环境中存在多种因素影响他们对生成式AI的使用，包括来自客户、老板和同行的压力；(3) 视觉艺术家普遍报告生成式AI对其工作场所产生负面影响，导致压力增加和工作机会减少。

Conclusion: 基于这些发现，作者鼓励HCI研究人员更深入地考虑艺术家不愿在工作场所使用生成式AI的意愿，并建议未来研究应更多关注艺术家对AI技术的抵制和拒绝策略。

Abstract: Generative AI has been heavily critiqued by artists in both popular media and HCI scholarship. However, more work is needed to understand the impacts of generative AI on professional artists' workplaces and careers. In this paper, we conduct a survey of \textit{378 verified professional visual artists} about how generative AI has impacted their careers and workplaces. We find (1) most visual artists are strongly opposed to using generative AI (text or visual) and negotiate their inclusion in the workplace through a variety of \textit{refusal} strategies (2) there exist a range of factors in artists environments shaping their use of generative AI, including pressure from clients, bosses, and peers and (3) visual artists report overwhelmingly negative impacts of generative AI on their workplaces, leading to added stress and reduced job opportunities. In light of these findings, we encourage HCI researchers to contend more deeply with artists' desires not to use generative AI in the workplace.

</details>


### [142] [Beyond the Interface: Redefining UX for Society-in-the-Loop AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04552)
*Nahal Mafi,Sahar Maleki,Babak Rahimi Ardabili,Hamed Tabkhi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个用于AI人机协同系统的社会技术用户体验框架，超越了传统的确定性系统UX概念，强调后端性能、组织工作流和决策结构的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统用户体验框架是为确定性系统设计的，无法捕捉AI人机协同系统中概率性输出和复杂社会技术动态带来的新挑战。AI系统在决策关键环境中运行，需要更全面的评估框架。

Method: 采用混合方法：1）对269个利益相关者见解进行归纳性社会建构分析；2）部署一个操作性的HITL视频异常检测系统进行实证研究。

Result: 研究发现利益相关者通过风险、治理和组织能力等多维度主题体验AI。实验结果显示检测行为和警报路由直接影响人类监督和工作负载。基于此，提出了包含四个社会技术指标的评估框架。

Conclusion: 论文形式化了一个新的评估框架，包含准确性、操作延迟、适应时间和信任四个核心指标，将UX重新定义为跨越基础设施和治理的多层结构，为复杂现实世界生态系统中的AI系统评估提供了严谨基础。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems increasingly operate in decision-critical environments where probabilistic outputs and Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) interactions reshape user engagement. Traditional user experience (UX) frameworks, designed for deterministic systems, fail to capture these evolving sociotechnical dynamics. This paper argues that in AI-enabled HITL systems, UX must transcend frontend usability to encompass backend performance, organizational workflows, and decision making structures.
  We employ a mixed-methods approach, combining an inductive social construction analysis of 269 stakeholder insights with the deployment of an operational HITL video anomaly detection system. Our findings reveal that stakeholders experience AI through multifaceted themes: risk, governance, and organizational capacity. Experimental results further demonstrate how detection behavior and alert routing directly calibrate human oversight and workload. Grounded in these results, we formalize a new evaluative framework centered on four sociotechnical metrics: Accuracy (FPR/FNR), Operational Latency (response time), Adaptation Time (deployment burden), and Trust (validated automation scales). This framework redefines UX as a multi-layered construct spanning infrastructure and governance, providing a rigorous foundation for evaluating AI systems embedded within complex real-world ecosystems.

</details>


### [143] [Beyond Anthropomorphism: a Spectrum of Interface Metaphors for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04613)
*Jianna So,Connie Cheng,Sonia Krishna Murthy*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出将拟人化重新定位为设计变量，开发从"反拟人化"到"超拟人化"的隐喻谱系，以打破LLM界面默认的拟人化倾向，促进用户批判性参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM界面过度强化拟人化隐喻，用户常将其视为真人，导致错觉和伤害。拟人化界面强调LLM与人类的相似性，却掩盖了关键差异，同时用户在使用伦理、普及性与有限界面选择之间存在认知失调。

Method: 将拟人化重新定位为设计变量，构建从透明度驱动的"反拟人化"到怪异的"超拟人化"的隐喻谱系。这些隐喻通过引入物质性，揭示LLM作为由人类劳动、基础设施和数据塑造的社会技术系统的本质。

Result: 提出了一种理论框架，展示界面隐喻如何塑造和打破默认隐喻。该谱系将界面设计从优化可用性转向鼓励批判性参与，为用户提供替代性的界面选择。

Conclusion: 通过重新定位拟人化为设计变量并开发隐喻谱系，可以打破LLM界面的默认拟人化倾向，促进用户对LLM作为社会技术系统的批判性理解，减少错觉和潜在伤害。

Abstract: Anthropomorphizing conversational technology is a natural human tendency. Today, the anthropomorphic metaphor is overly reinforced across intelligent tools. Large Language Models (LLMs) are particularly anthropomorphized through interface design. While metaphors are inherently partial, anthropomorphic interfaces highlight similarities between LLMs and humans, but mask crucial differences. As a result, the metaphor is often taken literally; users treat LLMs as if they are truly human. With few safeguards in place, this extreme anthropomorphism drives users to delusion and harm. Users also experience dissonance between the ethics of using LLMs, their growing ubiquity, and limited interface alternatives. We propose repositioning anthropomorphism as a design variable, developing opposing extremes as a theoretical framework for how interface metaphors shape and can disrupt the default metaphor. We introduce a spectrum of metaphors from transparency-driven ''anti-anthropomorphism'' to uncanny ''hyper-anthropomorphism''. These metaphors introduce materiality to interface metaphors, exposing LLMs as sociotechnical systems shaped by human labor, infrastructure, and data. This spectrum shifts interface design away from optimizing usability and toward encouraging critical engagement.

</details>


### [144] [Gamified Informed Decision-Making for Performance-Aware Design by Non-Experts: An Exoskeleton Design Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04643)
*Arman Khalilbeigi Khameneh,Armin Mostafavi,Alicia Nahmad Vazquez*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种集成游戏引擎与实时性能反馈的决策支持系统，用于帮助非专业设计师探索复杂的性能驱动设计空间，并在外骨骼立面改造项目中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策支持系统对于帮助非专业设计师探索复杂的性能驱动设计空间至关重要。现有工具往往缺乏直观的实时反馈机制，难以让用户理解结构行为、环境参数、制造和成本等多重性能标准之间的权衡关系。

Method: 开发了一个游戏化的决策制定框架，将游戏引擎与实时性能反馈集成。该框架允许用户迭代修改立面几何形状，同时接收三个关键标准的实时反馈：1)结构行为（挠度、质量、应力/强度比）；2)环境参数（太阳得热、供暖/制冷能耗）；3)制造考虑（制造成本、材料成本、机器人加工、材料设置）。通过24名参与者的实验验证框架效果。

Result: 参与者能够有效迭代修改立面几何形状并理解性能反馈。评估显示，游戏化反馈机制显著增强了用户对各项标准的理解和知情决策能力。参与者对结构、材料和制造性能的理解表明，精心策划的设计空间和结构化指导比开放式生成工具更高效。

Conclusion: 该研究为预占用评估做出了贡献，展示了游戏化环境如何通过知情决策和性能标准的定制化协商，使利益相关者能够参与设计过程。游戏化反馈机制能有效提升非专业设计师在复杂设计空间中的决策质量。

Abstract: Decision Support Systems (DSS) play a crucial role in enabling non-expert designers to explore complex, performance-driven design spaces. This paper presents a gamified decision-making framework that integrates game engines with real-time performance feedback. Performance criteria include structural behavior, environmental parameters, fabrication, material, and cost considerations. The developed design framework was tested with architecture students and non-expert designers on the design of an exoskeleton facade to retrofit an existing building. Participants (N=24) were able to iteratively modify façade geometries while receiving real-time feedback across the three key criteria: 1) structural behavior, including deflection, mass, and stress/strength ratio; 2) environmental parameters, such as solar gain and heating/cooling energy demands; and 3) fabrication considerations, including fabrication and material costs, robotic machining, and material setup. The evaluation of participant interactions reveals that gamified feedback mechanisms significantly enhance user comprehension and informed decision-making across the criteria. Further, participants' understanding of structural, material, and fabrication performance in relation to the iterative design task suggests that curated design spaces and structured guidance improve efficiency compared to open-ended generative tools. This research contributes to pre-occupancy evaluations, demonstrating how gamified environments enable stakeholder participation in the design process through informed decisionmaking and customized negotiation of performance criteria. .

</details>


### [145] [VizCrit: Exploring Strategies for Displaying Computational Feedback in a Visual Design Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04754)
*Mingyi Li,Mengyi Chen,Sarah Luo,Yining Cao,Haijun Xia,Maitraye Das,Steven P. Dow,Jane L. E*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VizCrit系统通过算法问题检测和视觉标注生成，为设计新手提供不同可操作性的反馈（教科书式、意识中心、解决方案中心），研究发现解决方案中心反馈能减少设计问题并提升自我感知创造力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉设计教学中多模态反馈（标注+文本）的重要性已被认识，但反馈的"可操作性"存在谱系差异——从提出相关设计概念到建议具体修复。需要探索创意工具如何实现支持这种反馈的标注，以及反馈可操作性如何影响新手的设计过程行为、创造力感知、设计原则学习和整体成果。

Method: 开发VizCrit系统，通过算法问题检测和视觉标注生成提供计算反馈，支持可操作性谱系。进行组间实验设计（N=36），新手在三种条件下修订设计：1)教科书式反馈（概念性），2)意识中心反馈（指出问题），3)解决方案中心反馈（具体修复建议）。

Result: 解决方案中心反馈相比教科书式反馈能显著减少设计问题并提高自我感知创造力，但专家对创造力的评分无显著差异。不同反馈类型对设计过程行为、学习效果和设计意识发展有不同影响。

Conclusion: AI创意支持工具应校准反馈可操作性，帮助新手在生产力与学习成长之间取得平衡。解决方案中心反馈能提升效率和自我效能感，但需考虑其对设计意识发展的影响。反馈可操作性的适当调整有助于新手在具体修复与概念理解之间找到平衡。

Abstract: Visual design instructors often provide multi-modal feedback, mixing annotations with text. Prior theory emphasizes the importance of actionable feedback, where "actionability" lies on a spectrum--from surfacing relevant design concepts to suggesting concrete fixes. How might creativity tools implement annotations that support such feedback, and how does the actionability of feedback impact novices' process-related behaviors, perceptions of creativity, learning of design principles, and overall outcomes? We introduce VizCrit, a system for providing computational feedback that supports the actionability spectrum, realized through algorithmic issue detection and visual annotation generation. In a between-subjects study (N=36), novices revised a design under one of three conditions: textbook-based, awareness-centered, or solution-centered feedback. We found that solution-centered feedback led to fewer design issues and higher self-perceived creativity compared with textbook-based feedback, although expert ratings on creativity showed no significant differences. We discuss the implications for AI in Creativity Support Tools, including the potential of calibrating feedback actionability to help novices balance productivity with learning, growth, and developing design awareness.

</details>


### [146] [Can LLMs Synthesize Court-Ready Statistical Evidence? Evaluating AI-Assisted Sentencing Bias Analysis for California Racial Justice Act Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04804)
*Aparna Komarla*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Redo.io是一个开源平台，通过处理加州监狱记录生成种族偏见量刑的法庭证据，并探索LLM驱动的解释层将统计结果转化为连贯叙述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管加州通过了《种族正义法案》(2020)等立法改革，允许被告基于种族差异的统计证据挑战定罪，但政策实施滞后于立法意图，导致大量重新量刑机会未被识别，形成"第二次机会差距"。

Method: 开发Redo.io开源平台，处理通过《加州公共记录法案》获取的95,000份监狱记录；使用比值比、相对风险、卡方检验等统计方法生成种族偏见证据；设计LLM驱动的解释层，将统计结果合成为包含置信区间、样本量和数据限制的连贯叙述；采用LLM-as-a-Judge框架评估LLM与统计学家的表现对比。

Result: 平台能够生成法庭就绪的种族偏见统计证据，用于初步证据和发现动议；评估表明AI可以作为强大的描述性助手，在伦理融入分析流程的情况下实现实时证据生成。

Conclusion: AI可以作为强大的描述性助手，在伦理融入分析流程的情况下，为实时证据生成提供支持，帮助弥合加州重新量刑中的"第二次机会差距"。

Abstract: Resentencing in California remains a complex legal challenge despite legislative reforms like the Racial Justice Act (2020), which allows defendants to challenge convictions based on statistical evidence of racial disparities in sentencing and charging. Policy implementation lags behind legislative intent, creating a 'second-chance gap' where hundreds of resentencing opportunities remain unidentified. We present Redo.io, an open-source platform that processes 95,000 prison records acquired under the California Public Records Act (CPRA) and generates court-ready statistical evidence of racial bias in sentencing for prima facie and discovery motions. We explore the design of an LLM-powered interpretive layer that synthesizes results from statistical methods like Odds Ratio, Relative Risk, and Chi-Square Tests into cohesive narratives contextualized with confidence intervals, sample sizes, and data limitations. Our evaluations comparing LLM performance to statisticians using the LLM-as-a-Judge framework suggest that AI can serve as a powerful descriptive assistant for real-time evidence generation when ethically incorporated in the analysis pipeline.

</details>


### [147] [SparkTales: Facilitating Cross-Language Collaborative Storytelling through Coordinator-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04806)
*Wenxin Zhao,Peng Zhang,Hansu Gu,Haoxuan Zhou,Xiaojie Huo,Lin Wang,Wen Zheng,Tun Lu,Ning Gu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: SparkTales是一个支持跨语言协作讲故事的智能系统，旨在减轻协调者负担并提升儿童参与度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言协作讲故事对儿童语言学习和文化发展很重要，但实践中儿童参与度浅，协调者面临认知和组织负担过重的问题

Method: 通过形成性研究识别协调者需求和痛点，设计SparkTales系统，利用儿童个体和共同特征提供故事框架、多样化问题和理解导向材料

Result: SparkTales显著提高了协调者的指导效率和质量，同时改善了儿童的参与度

Conclusion: 该系统为未来支持跨语言协作的智能系统设计提供了有价值的见解

Abstract: Cross-language collaborative storytelling plays a vital role in children's language learning and cultural development, fostering both expressive ability and intercultural awareness. Yet, in practice, children's participation is often shallow, and facilitating such sessions places heavy cognitive and organizational burdens on coordinators, who must coordinate language support, maintain children's engagement, and navigate cultural differences. To address these challenges, we conducted a formative study with coordinators to identify their needs and pain points, which guided the design of SparkTales, an intelligent support system for cross-language collaborative storytelling. SparkTales leverages both individual and common characteristics of participating children to provide coordinators with story frameworks, diverse questions, and comprehension-oriented materials, aiming to reduce coordinators' workload while enhancing children's interactive engagement. Evaluation results show that SparkTales not only significantly increases coordinators' efficiency and quality of guidance but also improves children's participation, providing valuable insights for the design of future intelligent systems supporting cross-language collaboration.

</details>


### [148] [Roomify: Spatially-Grounded Style Transformation for Immersive Virtual Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04917)
*Xueyang Wang,Qinxuan Cen,Weitao Bi,Yunxiang Ma,Xin Yi,Robert Xiao,Xinyi Fu,Hewu Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Roomify是一个空间锚定的虚拟环境生成系统，将用户的物理房间转换为风格化虚拟环境，同时保持空间结构和功能语义，解决了VR中沉浸感与空间感知的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VR方法面临基本权衡：完全沉浸会牺牲空间感知，而透视解决方案会破坏临场感。需要一种既能保持空间意识又能提供沉浸体验的解决方案。

Method: 采用空间锚定转换方法，将物理空间视为"空间容器"，保持家具的关键功能和几何属性，同时实现激进风格变化。结合现场3D场景理解、AI驱动的空间推理和风格感知生成，创建基于物理现实的个性化虚拟环境。开发了跨现实创作工具，支持MR编辑和VR预览工作流。

Result: 用户研究显示：18名VR用户的临场感比透视方案提高63%，比完全虚拟基线提高26%，同时保持空间感知。8名设计专业人员确认系统的创意表现力（场景质量：5.95/7；创意支持：6.08/7）和跨多样化环境的专业工作流价值。

Conclusion: Roomify通过空间锚定转换成功解决了VR中沉浸感与空间感知的权衡问题，提供了一种既能保持物理空间功能语义又能实现风格化沉浸体验的创新方法，在用户体验和创意表达方面都表现出色。

Abstract: We present Roomify, a spatially-grounded transformation system that generates themed virtual environments anchored to users' physical rooms while maintaining spatial structure and functional semantics. Current VR approaches face a fundamental trade-off: full immersion sacrifices spatial awareness, while passthrough solutions break presence. Roomify addresses this through spatially-grounded transformation - treating physical spaces as "spatial containers" that preserve key functional and geometric properties of furniture while enabling radical stylistic changes. Our pipeline combines in-situ 3D scene understanding, AI-driven spatial reasoning, and style-aware generation to create personalized virtual environments grounded in physical reality. We introduce a cross-reality authoring tool enabling fine-grained user control through MR editing and VR preview workflows. Two user studies validate our approach: one with 18 VR users demonstrates a 63% improvement in presence over passthrough and 26% over fully virtual baselines while maintaining spatial awareness; another with 8 design professionals confirms the system's creative expressiveness (scene quality: 5.95/7; creativity support: 6.08/7) and professional workflow value across diverse environments.

</details>


### [149] [Mind the Gap: Mapping Wearer-Bystander Privacy Tensions and Context-Adaptive Pathways for Camera Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04930)
*Xueyang Wang,Kewen Peng,Xin Yi,Hewu Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过调查和访谈系统评估了智能眼镜的隐私机制，揭示了佩戴者与旁观者之间的期望差距，识别了隐私增强技术的四个基本权衡，并提出了基于上下文的自适应保护路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能眼镜在佩戴者（希望记录功能）和旁观者（担心未经授权的监控）之间造成了根本的隐私紧张关系。现有研究缺乏对多利益相关方隐私机制的系统评估，特别是在不同情境下的接受度差异。

Method: 采用混合方法：1）对中国525名参与者进行问卷调查，量化期望-意愿差距；2）对20名参与者进行配对访谈，深入评估12种隐私增强技术。研究分析了不同情境下的隐私接受度差异。

Result: 研究1发现旁观者始终要求比佩戴者愿意提供的更强的信息透明度和保护措施，在敏感情境下差距加剧（65-90%的旁观者会采取防御行动）。研究2揭示了隐私增强技术的四个基本权衡：可见性vs干扰、赋权vs负担、保护vs自主权、问责vs暴露。上下文是隐私可接受性的主要决定因素。

Conclusion: 隐私差距反映了结构性不相容而非善意不足。研究者提出了上下文自适应路径：公共场所采用最小摩擦的可见性，半公共环境采用结构化协商，敏感情境采用自动保护。这为评估隐私机制和上下文感知设计提供了诊断框架。

Abstract: Camera glasses create fundamental privacy tensions between wearers seeking recording functionality and bystanders concerned about unauthorized surveillance. We present a systematic multi-stakeholder evaluation of privacy mechanisms through surveys (N=525) and paired interviews (N=20) in China. Study 1 quantifies expectation-willingness gaps: bystanders consistently demand stronger information transparency and protective measures than wearers will provide, with disparities intensifying in sensitive contexts where 65-90% of bystanders would take defensive action. Study 2 evaluates twelve privacy-enhancing technologies, revealing four fundamental trade-offs that undermine current approaches: visibility versus disruption, empowerment versus burden, protection versus agency, and accountability versus exposure. These gaps reflect structural incompatibilities rather than inadequate goodwill, with context emerging as the primary determinant of privacy acceptability. We propose context-adaptive pathways that dynamically adjust protection strategies: minimal-friction visibility in public spaces, structured negotiation in semi-public environments, and automatic protection in sensitive contexts. Our findings contribute a diagnostic framework for evaluating privacy mechanisms and implications for context-aware design in ubiquitous sensing.

</details>


### [150] [Beyond Advocacy: A Design Space for Replication-Related Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04959)
*Yiheng Liang,Kim Marriott,Helen C. Purchase*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一个用于复制实验设计的四维度三层次设计空间框架，支持复制研究的决策制定、分类、比较和分析


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复制研究在科学领域（包括可视化和HCI）中至关重要，但在进行复制时需要决定哪些方面保持不变、哪些需要改变，目前缺乏系统化的决策支持框架

Method: 将复制实验设计视为成对比较问题，提出一个包含四个实践维度的设计空间框架，每个维度由三个比较层次定义，支持回顾性特征描述和前瞻性规划

Result: 开发了一个实用的多维度设计空间框架，提供了工作示例，并将该框架与其他描述复制研究范围的方法进行了关联

Conclusion: 该框架为复制研究的决策制定提供了系统化支持，能够帮助研究者识别、分类、比较和分析复制设计中的关键要素，促进更严谨和透明的复制研究实践

Abstract: The importance of replication is often discussed and advocated -- not only in the domains of visualization and HCI, but in all scientific areas. When replicating a study, design decisions need to be made with regards which aspects of the original study will remain the same and which will be altered. We present a supporting multi-dimensional design space framework within which such decisions can be identified, categorized, compared and analyzed. The framework treats replication experimental design as a pairwise comparison problem, and represents the design by four practical dimensions defined by three comparison levels. The design space is therefore a framework that can be used for both retrospective characterization and prospective planning. We provide worked examples, and relate our framework to other attempts at describing the scope of replication studies.

</details>


### [151] [Auto-Generating Personas from User Reviews in VR App Stores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04985)
*Yi Wang,Kexin Cheng,Xiao Liu,Chetan Arora,John Grundy,Thuong Hoang,Henry Been-Lirn Duh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究开发了VR课程中的自动生成人物角色系统，用于促进无障碍需求讨论和VR设计开发


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人物角色是讨论软件设计中无障碍需求的有价值工具，但在VR项目中用于无障碍需求获取的应用有限且面临挑战

Method: 在VR课程中开发自动生成人物角色系统，利用这些角色促进无障碍需求讨论并指导VR设计和开发

Result: 自动生成人物角色系统使学生能够更有效地培养同理心

Conclusion: 该研究展示了在VR课程环境中使用自动生成人物角色作为获取潜在无障碍需求的手段

Abstract: Personas are a valuable tool for discussing accessibility requirements in software design and development practices. However, the use of personas for accessibility-focused requirements elicitation in VR projects remains limited and is accompanied by several challenges. To fill this gap, we developed an auto-generated persona system in a VR course, where the personas were used to facilitate discussions on accessibility requirements and to guide VR design and development. Our findings indicate that the auto-generated persona system enabled students to develop empathy more efficiently. This study demonstrates the use of automatically generated personas in VR course settings as a means of eliciting latent accessibility requirements.

</details>


### [152] [Haptics in Cognition: Disruptor or Enabler of Memory?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05019)
*Bibeg Limbu,Irene-Angelica Chounta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 探索性试点研究调查触觉感知（触觉敏感性和动觉强度）对通过手写进行信息保留（即时回忆）学习的影响，发现增加书写压力可能轻微损害回忆，而单独使用手套无影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究触觉感知（触觉敏感性和动觉强度）如何影响学习，特别是通过手写进行的信息保留，旨在理解具身交互与认知结果之间的复杂关系。

Method: 采用2x2因子设计的探索性试点研究，N=20名参与者随机分配到四个实验组，操纵触觉（通过手套使用）和动觉（通过增加书写压力）。测量信息保留（即时回忆测试）、心理努力（次要任务反应时间）和感知工作量（NASA-TLX）作为中介变量，使用贝叶斯二项回归和中介分析。

Result: 贝叶斯二项回归显示中等证据表明增加书写压力对回忆有负面影响（85-88%概率为负效应），而单独使用手套无明确影响。贝叶斯中介分析未发现心理努力或感知工作量中介这些效应的强证据，所有95%可信区间包含零，表明不确定性较大。
