Conclusion: 增加动觉需求可能轻微损害即时回忆，且独立于感知工作量或心理努力；单独操纵触觉不影响信息保留。研究有助于理解具身交互与认知结果的微妙关系，对设计基于传感器的多模态学习环境有启示。

Abstract: This exploratory pilot study investigates the impact of haptic perception --specifically tactile sensitivity (touch) and kinaesthetic intensity (movement)-- on learning, operationalized as information retention (immediate recall) through handwriting. Participants (N=20) were randomly assigned to one of four experimental groups in a 2x2 factorial design, manipulating touch (via glove use) and movement (via increased writing pressure). Information retention was measured using an immediate recall test, while mental effort (reaction time in a secondary task) and perceived workload (NASA-TLX) were examined as mediating variables. Bayesian binomial regression revealed moderate evidence that increased writing pressure negatively influenced recall (85-88% probability of negative effect), whereas glove use alone demonstrated no clear effect. Bayesian mediation analysis found no strong evidence that mental effort or perceived workload mediated these effects, as all 95% credible intervals included zero, indicating substantial uncertainty. These findings suggest that increased Kinaesthetic demands may slightly impair immediate recall, independent of perceived workload or mental effort. Importantly, the manipulation of touch alone does not appear to influence information retention. The study contributes to understanding the nuanced relationship between embodied interactions and cognitive outcomes, with implications for designing sensor-based multimodal learning environments.

</details>


### [153] [Wire Your Way: Hardware-Contextualized Guidance and In-situ Tests for Personalized Circuit Prototyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05085)
*Punn Lertjaturaphat,Jungwoo Rhee,Jaewon You,Andrea Bianchi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发了一个支持个性化电路构建和调试的原型平台，通过增强型面包板提供电路感知、上下文引导和原位验证功能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统教程和工具包采用逐步指导的方式，与创客在电路构建和调试方面的个性化偏好存在不匹配，需要支持更灵活的个性化工作流程

Method: 开发了电路感知的增强型面包板系统，支持实时硬件重新配置（通过上下文引导）和原位电路验证（通过交互式测试）

Result: 可用性研究（N=12）表明创客能够利用电路感知引导和调试功能来支持个性化的构建模式

Conclusion: 提出的原型平台通过电路感知引导和调试功能，有效支持创客的个性化电路构建需求，解决了传统教程方法与创客实际工作方式之间的不匹配问题

Abstract: The increasing popularity of microcontroller platforms like Arduino enables diverse end-user developers to participate in circuit prototyping. Traditionally, follow-along tutorials serve as an essential learning method for makers, and in fact, several prior toolkits leveraged this format as a way to engage new makers. However, literature and our formative study (N=12) show that makers have unique preferences regarding the construction of their circuits and idiosyncratic ways to assess and debug problems, which contrasts with the step-by-step instructional nature of tutorials and those systems leveraging this method. To address this mismatch, we present a prototyping platform that supports personalized circuit construction and debugging. Our system utilizes an augmented breadboard, which is circuit-aware and supports on-the-fly hardware reconfiguration via contextualized guidance and in-situ circuit validation through interactive tests. Through a usability study (N=12), we demonstrate how makers leverage circuit-aware guidance and debugging to support individual building patterns.

</details>


### [154] [Not All Trust is the Same: Effects of Decision Workflow and Explanations in Human-AI Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05229)
*Laura Spillner,Rachel Ringe,Robert Porzel,Rainer Malaka*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究比较了AI辅助决策中两种工作流程（一步式与两步式）对信任校准的影响，发现两步式设置并未减少过度依赖，且工作流程与解释、领域知识存在交互效应，证实了报告信任与行为信任是不同的构念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助决策中的核心挑战是实现合理、校准良好的信任，既要防止过度信任（接受错误的AI建议），也要防止信任不足（拒绝正确的建议）。先前研究在工作流程设计（一步式vs两步式）和信任测量方式（自我报告vs行为信任）上存在差异，需要系统考察这些因素及其交互作用。

Method: 研究考察了三个因素：(a)决策工作流程类型（一步式vs两步式），(b)解释的存在与否，(c)用户的领域知识和先前AI经验。比较了报告信任、行为信任（同意率和切换率）以及过度依赖等指标。

Result: 结果显示：1）两步式设置并未减少过度依赖；2）决策工作流程不直接影响自我报告信任，但与领域知识和解释存在交叉交互效应，表明解释的效果可能无法跨工作流程推广；3）报告信任与行为信任是不同的构念，应分开评估。

Conclusion: AI辅助决策中，工作流程设计对信任校准的影响复杂，两步式设置并非减少过度依赖的通用解决方案。解释的效果受工作流程影响，且信任测量需要区分自我报告和行为表现。未来研究应考虑这些因素间的交互作用。

Abstract: A central challenge in AI-assisted decision making is achieving warranted, well-calibrated trust. Both overtrust (accepting incorrect AI recommendations) and undertrust (rejecting correct advice) should be prevented. Prior studies differ in the design of the decision workflow - whether users see the AI suggestion immediately (1-step setup) or have to submit a first decision beforehand (2-step setup) -, and in how trust is measured - through self-reports or as behavioral trust, that is, reliance. We examined the effects and interactions of (a) the type of decision workflow, (b) the presence of explanations, and (c) users' domain knowledge and prior AI experience. We compared reported trust, reliance (agreement rate and switch rate), and overreliance. Results showed no evidence that a 2-step setup reduces overreliance. The decision workflow also did not directly affect self-reported trust, but there was a crossover interaction effect with domain knowledge and explanations, suggesting that the effects of explanations alone may not generalize across workflow setups. Finally, our findings confirm that reported trust and reliance behavior are distinct constructs that should be evaluated separately in AI-assisted decision making.

</details>


### [155] [AttentiveLearn: Personalized Post-Lecture Support for Gaze-Aware Immersive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05324)
*Shi Liu,Martin Feick,Linus Bierhoff,Alexander Maedche*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AttentiveLearn：基于VR眼动追踪的个性化课后测验系统，通过分析学习者在VR课堂中的注意力分布，在移动学习助手上生成个性化测验，提升学习动机和参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式学习环境（如VR虚拟教室）为学习者提供了独特的学习体验，但有效的学习者支持仍然是一个挑战。虽然先前的人机交互研究探索了沉浸式学习中的课堂内支持，但课后支持的研究很少，尽管这对于维持学习动机、参与度和学习成果至关重要。

Method: 提出AttentiveLearn学习生态系统，该系统通过VR课堂中的眼动追踪推断学习者的注意力分布，基于此在移动学习助手上生成个性化测验。在为期四周的实地研究中，36名大学生参加了贝叶斯数据分析课程，评估了该系统的效果。

Result: AttentiveLearn提高了学习者报告的学习动机和参与度，但没有确凿证据表明学习成果有显著提升。同时，轶事证据表明某些参与者的注意力随时间有所改善。研究为沉浸式学习系统的个性化课后支持提供了实证见解和设计启示。

Conclusion: AttentiveLearn展示了基于眼动追踪的个性化课后支持系统在提升VR学习动机和参与度方面的潜力，为沉浸式学习系统的课后支持设计提供了重要参考，尽管学习成果的提升需要进一步验证。

Abstract: Immersive learning environments such as virtual classrooms in Virtual Reality (VR) offer learners unique learning experiences, yet providing effective learner support remains a challenge. While prior HCI research has explored in-lecture support for immersive learning, little research has been conducted to provide post-lecture support, despite being critical for sustained motivation, engagement, and learning outcomes. To address this, we present AttentiveLearn, a learning ecosystem that generates personalized quizzes on a mobile learning assistant based on learners' attention distribution inferred using eye-tracking in VR lectures. We evaluated the system in a four-week field study with 36 university students attending lectures on Bayesian data analysis. AttentiveLearn improved learners' reported motivation and engagement, without conclusive evidence of learning gains. Meanwhile, anecdotal evidence suggested improvements in attention for certain participants over time. Based on our findings of the field study, we provide empirical insights and design implications for personalized post-lecture support for immersive learning systems.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Dictionary Based Pattern Entropy for Causal Direction Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04473)
*Harikrishnan N B,Shubham Bhilare,Aditi Kathpalia,Nithin Nagaraj*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出基于字典模式熵（DPE）的因果方向推断框架，结合算法信息论和香农信息论，通过最小化模式级不确定性来发现符号序列中的因果关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间观测数据中的符号序列因果方向发现具有挑战性，因为缺乏功能模型和噪声假设。需要一种能够识别因果方向并解释驱动变化的特定子模式的框架。

Method: 提出字典模式熵（DPE）框架，将因果关系解释为候选原因中紧凑、基于规则的模式系统地约束效应的出现。构建方向特定字典，使用基于熵的度量量化其影响，通过最小不确定性准则推断因果方向。

Result: DPE在多种合成系统（延迟比特翻转扰动、AR(1)耦合、1D斜帐篷映射、稀疏过程）中始终实现可靠性能，优于或匹配竞争性AIT方法（ETC_E、ETC_P、LZ_P）。在生物和生态数据集中表现有竞争力。

Conclusion: 最小化模式级不确定性提供了一个稳健、可解释且广泛适用的因果发现框架，能够有效推断符号序列中的因果方向并识别驱动变化的特定子模式。

Abstract: Discovering causal direction from temporal observational data is particularly challenging for symbolic sequences, where functional models and noise assumptions are often unavailable. We propose a novel \emph{Dictionary Based Pattern Entropy ($DPE$)} framework that infers both the direction of causation and the specific subpatterns driving changes in the effect variable. The framework integrates \emph{Algorithmic Information Theory} (AIT) and \emph{Shannon Information Theory}. Causation is interpreted as the emergence of compact, rule based patterns in the candidate cause that systematically constrain the effect. $DPE$ constructs direction-specific dictionaries and quantifies their influence using entropy-based measures, enabling a principled link between deterministic pattern structure and stochastic variability. Causal direction is inferred via a minimum-uncertainty criterion, selecting the direction exhibiting stronger and more consistent pattern-driven organization. As summarized in Table 7, $DPE$ consistently achieves reliable performance across diverse synthetic systems, including delayed bit-flip perturbations, AR(1) coupling, 1D skew-tent maps, and sparse processes, outperforming or matching competing AIT-based methods ($ETC_E$, $ETC_P$, $LZ_P$). In biological and ecological datasets, performance is competitive, while alternative methods show advantages in specific genomic settings. Overall, the results demonstrate that minimizing pattern level uncertainty yields a robust, interpretable, and broadly applicable framework for causal discovery.

</details>


### [157] [Bayesian Modeling of Collatz Stopping Times: A Probabilistic Machine Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04479)
*Nicolò Bonacorsi,Matteo Bordoni*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 使用贝叶斯负二项回归和基于奇块分解的生成模型，从概率机器学习角度研究Collatz总停止时间τ(n)的分布特性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从概率机器学习视角研究Collatz总停止时间τ(n)的统计特性，τ(n)表现出明显的偏斜、过度离散和算术异质性，需要开发能够捕捉这些特性的概率模型

Method: 开发了两种互补模型：1）贝叶斯分层负二项回归(NB2-GLM)，使用log n和n mod 8作为协变量预测τ(n)；2）基于奇块分解的机制生成近似模型，通过随机化块长度并使用Dirichlet-multinomial更新进行校准

Result: 在n≤10^7范围内，NB2-GLM在预测似然度上显著优于奇块生成模型；将块长度分布条件化于m mod 8后，生成模型的分布拟合显著改善，表明低阶模结构是τ(n)异质性的关键驱动因素

Conclusion: Collatz总停止时间的分布具有显著的算术异质性，低阶模结构（特别是mod 8）是理解其分布的关键；贝叶斯回归模型在预测性能上优于机制生成模型，但两者结合可提供互补的视角

Abstract: We study the Collatz total stopping time $τ(n)$ over $n\le 10^7$ from a probabilistic machine learning viewpoint. Empirically, $τ(n)$ is a skewed and heavily overdispersed count with pronounced arithmetic heterogeneity. We develop two complementary models. First, a Bayesian hierarchical Negative Binomial regression (NB2-GLM) predicts $τ(n)$ from simple covariates ($\log n$ and residue class $n \bmod 8$), quantifying uncertainty via posterior and posterior predictive distributions. Second, we propose a mechanistic generative approximation based on the odd-block decomposition: for odd $m$, write $3m+1=2^{K(m)}m'$ with $m'$ odd and $K(m)=v_2(3m+1)\ge 1$; randomizing these block lengths yields a stochastic approximation calibrated via a Dirichlet-multinomial update. On held-out data, the NB2-GLM achieves substantially higher predictive likelihood than the odd-block generators. Conditioning the block-length distribution on $m\bmod 8$ markedly improves the generator's distributional fit, indicating that low-order modular structure is a key driver of heterogeneity in $τ(n)$.

</details>


### [158] [The Volterra signature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04525)
*Paul P. Hager,Fabian N. Harang,Luca Pelizzari,Samy Tindel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出Volterra签名作为历史依赖系统的显式特征表示，通过张量代数中的时间核加权路径开发，提供可解释性、理论保证和计算可行性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非马尔可夫时间序列学习方法（如RNN、神经控制微分方程、Transformer）依赖隐式记忆机制，存在可解释性差和长时训练困难的问题，需要一种原则性的显式特征表示

Method: 提出Volterra签名VSig(x;K)，通过时间核K对输入路径x进行加权，在张量代数中展开，利用Volterra-Chen恒等式建立理论框架，支持核技巧和线性状态空间ODE表示

Result: 证明了注入性（增强下的可识别性）和无限维路径空间上的通用逼近定理，对指数型核类，Volterra签名可解为张量代数中的线性ODE，在真实和合成数据上优于经典路径签名基线

Conclusion: Volterra签名作为鲁棒、计算可行的特征映射，结合时间重参数化不变性，为历史依赖系统提供了原则性的显式表示方法，在动态学习任务中表现优异

Abstract: Modern approaches for learning from non-Markovian time series, such as recurrent neural networks, neural controlled differential equations or transformers, typically rely on implicit memory mechanisms that can be difficult to interpret or to train over long horizons. We propose the Volterra signature $\mathrm{VSig}(x;K)$ as a principled, explicit feature representation for history-dependent systems. By developing the input path $x$ weighted by a temporal kernel $K$ into the tensor algebra, we leverage the associated Volterra--Chen identity to derive rigorous learning-theoretic guarantees. Specifically, we prove an injectivity statement (identifiability under augmentation) that leads to a universal approximation theorem on the infinite dimensional path space, which in certain cases is achieved by linear functionals of $\mathrm{VSig}(x;K)$. Moreover, we demonstrate applicability of the kernel trick by showing that the inner product associated with Volterra signatures admits a closed characterization via a two-parameter integral equation, enabling numerical methods from PDEs for computation. For a large class of exponential-type kernels, $\mathrm{VSig}(x;K)$ solves a linear state-space ODE in the tensor algebra. Combined with inherent invariance to time reparameterization, these results position the Volterra signature as a robust, computationally tractable feature map for data science. We demonstrate its efficacy in dynamic learning tasks on real and synthetic data, where it consistently improves classical path signature baselines.

</details>


### [159] [Optimal Prediction-Augmented Algorithms for Testing Independence of Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04635)
*Maryam Aliakbarpour,Alireza Azizi,Ria Stevens*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种增强分布测试框架下的独立性检验方法，通过整合辅助预测信息来提升样本效率，同时保持最坏情况下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的非参数有限样本独立性检验存在样本复杂度随支持度多项式增长的瓶颈问题，作者希望通过利用预测信息来超越这些最坏情况限制。

Method: 设计了增强分布测试框架，包含：(1) 针对离散分布的双变量独立性检验器，能根据预测误差自适应降低样本复杂度；(2) 扩展到高维多变量设置，检验d个随机变量的独立性；(3) 建立了匹配的极小极大下界。

Result: 提出的检验器在预测准确时显著提高样本效率，同时无论预测质量如何都保持最坏情况有效性，实现了最优样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 通过整合预测信息，本文超越了传统独立性检验的最坏情况限制，在保持鲁棒性的同时显著提升了样本效率，为统计推断提供了更实用的工具。

Abstract: Independence testing is a fundamental problem in statistical inference: given samples from a joint distribution $p$ over multiple random variables, the goal is to determine whether $p$ is a product distribution or is $ε$-far from all product distributions in total variation distance. In the non-parametric finite-sample regime, this task is notoriously expensive, as the minimax sample complexity scales polynomially with the support size. In this work, we move beyond these worst-case limitations by leveraging the framework of \textit{augmented distribution testing}. We design independence testers that incorporate auxiliary, but potentially untrustworthy, predictive information. Our framework ensures that the tester remains robust, maintaining worst-case validity regardless of the prediction's quality, while significantly improving sample efficiency when the prediction is accurate. Our main contributions include: (i) a bivariate independence tester for discrete distributions that adaptively reduces sample complexity based on the prediction error; (ii) a generalization to the high-dimensional multivariate setting for testing the independence of $d$ random variables; and (iii) matching minimax lower bounds demonstrating that our testers achieve optimal sample complexity.

</details>


### [160] [Learning Optimal Individualized Decision Rules with Conditional Demographic Parity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05226)
*Wenhai Cui,Wen Su,Donglin Zeng,Xingqiu Zhao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一个将人口统计均等和条件人口统计均等约束纳入最优个体化决策规则估计的新框架，通过扰动无约束最优IDR实现高效计算


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体化决策规则在个性化营销、医疗保健和公共政策设计等社会应用中日益普遍，但基于偏见数据训练的IDR可能对性别、种族或语言等敏感属性定义的少数亚群体造成不成比例的伤害，存在歧视性影响

Method: 提出将人口统计均等和条件人口统计均等约束纳入最优个体化决策规则估计的新框架，证明在DP和CDP约束下的理论最优IDR可以通过对无约束最优IDR应用扰动获得，从而实现计算高效的解决方案

Result: 理论上推导了策略价值和公平约束项的收敛率，通过综合模拟研究和俄勒冈健康保险实验的实证应用证明了方法的有效性

Conclusion: 该框架为解决个体化决策规则中的公平性问题提供了理论保证和实用方法，能够在保证公平性的同时实现高效计算

Abstract: Individualized decision rules (IDRs) have become increasingly prevalent in societal applications such as personalized marketing, healthcare, and public policy design. However, a critical ethical concern arises from the potential discriminatory effects of IDRs trained on biased data. These algorithms may disproportionately harm individuals from minority subgroups defined by sensitive attributes like gender, race, or language. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework that incorporates demographic parity (DP) and conditional demographic parity (CDP) constraints into the estimation of optimal IDRs. We show that the theoretically optimal IDRs under DP and CDP constraints can be obtained by applying perturbations to the unconstrained optimal IDRs, enabling a computationally efficient solution. Theoretically, we derive convergence rates for both policy value and the fairness constraint term. The effectiveness of our methods is illustrated through comprehensive simulation studies and an empirical application to the Oregon Health Insurance Experiment.

</details>


### [161] [Bayesian Supervised Causal Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05288)
*Luwei Wang,Nazir Lone,Sohan Seth*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯监督因果聚类(BSCC)方法，以处理效应为结果指导聚类过程，识别协变量特征和处理效应均相似的个体亚组


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗健康和政策评估等领域，识别具有相似特征的亚组对于个性化决策至关重要。现有方法多依赖无监督聚类，但监督聚类方法能针对特定关注结果识别可操作的亚组，具有优势。

Method: 提出贝叶斯监督因果聚类(BSCC)方法，以处理效应作为结果指导聚类过程。该方法识别协变量特征和处理效应均相似的个体亚组。

Result: 在模拟数据集和真实世界数据集（第三次国际卒中试验）上评估BSCC，验证了该框架的实际有用性。

Conclusion: BSCC为识别具有相似特征和处理效应的患者亚组提供了一种有效的监督聚类方法，有助于个性化决策制定。

Abstract: Finding patient subgroups with similar characteristics is crucial for personalized decision-making in various disciplines such as healthcare and policy evaluation. While most existing approaches rely on unsupervised clustering methods, there is a growing trend toward using supervised clustering methods that identify operationalizable subgroups in the context of a specific outcome of interest. We propose Bayesian Supervised Causal Clustering (BSCC), with treatment effect as outcome to guide the clustering process. BSCC identifies homogenous subgroups of individuals who are similar in their covariate profiles as well as their treatment effects. We evaluate BSCC on simulated datasets as well as real-world dataset from the third International Stroke Trial to assess the practical usefulness of the framework.

</details>


### [162] [Bayes with No Shame: Admissibility Geometries of Predictive Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05335)
*Nicholas G. Polson,Daniel Zantedeschi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文提出了四种不同的可容许性几何框架，用于序列和无分布推断，并证明了它们之间的分离定理：这四类可容许程序是两两不嵌套的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索序列和无分布推断中不同可容许性标准之间的几何关系。现有文献中存在多种可容许性概念，但缺乏对这些概念之间关系的系统比较。作者旨在阐明这些不同几何框架的差异，证明它们之间的分离性，从而揭示可容许性本质上是准则相对的这一核心观点。

Method: 研究方法基于几何分析框架，定义了四种可容许性几何：1) Blackwell风险支配于凸风险集；2) 非负上鞅锥内的随时有效可容许性；3) 可交换预测集上的边际覆盖有效性；4) Cesàro可接近性(CAA)可容许性。通过证明分离定理，展示这四类可容许程序是两两不嵌套的。每种几何都有不同的最优性证书：支持超平面先验、非负上鞅、可交换秩或Cesàro引导论证。

Result: 主要结果包括：1) 证明了分离定理，四种可容许性类别是两两不嵌套的；2) 鞅相干性对Blackwell可容许性是必要的，对e-过程中的随时有效可容许性是必要且充分的，但对Blackwell可容许性不充分，对覆盖有效性或CAA可容许性不必要；3) 所有四种准则共享共同的优化模板，但约束集在不同空间、偏序和性能度量上操作，导致几何不兼容；4) 可容许性本质上是准则相对的。

Conclusion: 结论是序列和无分布推断中存在四种不同的可容许性几何框架，它们之间具有本质差异。可容许性不是绝对的，而是相对于特定准则的。不同框架使用不同的最优性证书和几何结构，无法统一到一个单一的可容许性概念下。这一发现对统计推断理论具有重要启示，强调了选择适当可容许性准则的重要性。

Abstract: Four distinct admissibility geometries govern sequential and distribution-free inference: Blackwell risk dominance over convex risk sets, anytime-valid admissibility within the nonnegative supermartingale cone, marginal coverage validity over exchangeable prediction sets, and Cesàro approachability (CAA) admissibility, which reaches the risk-set boundary via approachability-style arguments rather than explicit priors. We prove a criterion separation theorem: the four classes of admissible procedures are pairwise non-nested. Each geometry carries a different certificate of optimality: a supporting-hyperplane prior (Blackwell), a nonnegative supermartingale (anytime-valid), an exchangeability rank (coverage), or a Cesàro steering argument (CAA). Martingale coherence is necessary for Blackwell admissibility and necessary and sufficient for anytime-valid admissibility within e-processes, but is not sufficient for Blackwell admissibility and is not necessary for coverage validity or CAA-admissibility. All four criteria share a common optimization template (minimize Bayesian risk subject to a feasibility constraint), but the constraint sets operate over different spaces, partial orders, and performance metrics, making them geometrically incompatible. Admissibility is irreducibly criterion-relative.

</details>


### [163] [How important are the genes to explain the outcome - the asymmetric Shapley value as an honest importance metric for high-dimensional features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05317)
*Mark A. van de Wiel,Jeroen Goedhart,Martin Jullum,Kjersti Aas*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出使用非对称Shapley值作为量化混合维度预测模型中特征重要性的方法，特别适用于临床预测中疾病状态作为基因组效应中介变量的场景


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统临床预测中评估高维特征（如基因组学）重要性时，通常通过将其添加到传统临床变量集后观察预测性能变化，但这种方法未考虑共线性问题，也未考虑变量间依赖关系的已知方向性

Method: 提出使用非对称Shapley值来量化混合维度预测模型中的特征重要性，特别关注疾病状态作为基因组效应中介变量且存在未知方向性混杂因素的临床预测场景，推导了计算局部和全局非对称Shapley值的高效算法

Result: 局部非对称Shapley值对推断非常有用，而全局非对称Shapley值通过将任何预测性能指标分解为特征贡献来提供解释，以结直肠癌患者无进展生存期预测为例进行了框架说明

Conclusion: 非对称Shapley值是量化混合维度预测模型中特征重要性的更合适替代方法，能够解决传统方法中未考虑的共线性和变量间依赖关系方向性问题

Abstract: In clinical prediction settings the importance of a high-dimensional feature like genomics is often assessed by evaluating the change in predictive performance when adding it to a set of traditional clinical variables. This approach is questionable, because it does not account for collinearity nor known directionality of dependencies between variables. We suggest to use asymmetric Shapley values as a more suitable alternative to quantify feature importance in the context of a mixed-dimensional prediction model. We focus on a setting that is particularly relevant in clinical prediction: disease state as a mediating variable for genomic effects, with additional confounders for which the direction of effects may be unknown. We derive efficient algorithms to compute local and global asymmetric Shapley values for this setting. The former are shown to be very useful for inference, whereas the latter provide interpretation by decomposing any predictive performance metric into contributions of the features. Throughout, we illustrate our framework by a leading example: the prediction of progression-free survival for colorectal cancer patients.

</details>


### [164] [On the Statistical Optimality of Optimal Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05340)
*Zineng Xu,Subhroshekhar Ghosh,Yan Shuo Tan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文为经验风险最小化（ERM）决策树建立了全面的统计理论，包括高维回归和分类中的尖锐oracle不等式、最小化最优速率，并引入了一种新的函数类（PSHAB空间）来捕捉稀疏性、各向异性平滑度和空间异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管全局最优ERM决策树在计算上已可行且经验上成功，但其统计性能的严格理论保证仍然有限。需要为ERM树建立全面的统计理论，特别是在高维回归和分类的随机设计下。

Method: 1. 使用基于经验局部化Rademacher复杂度的新颖均匀集中框架建立尖锐oracle不等式；2. 推导新函数类（PSHAB空间）上的最小化最优速率；3. 在亚高斯性假设下建立主要结果，并在重尾噪声设置下提供鲁棒保证。

Result: 1. 建立了ERM估计器相对于最多L个叶子的最佳可能树逼近的过剩风险界限，刻画了可解释性-准确性权衡；2. 在PSHAB空间上获得了最小化最优速率；3. 提供了亚高斯和重尾噪声设置下的统计保证。

Conclusion: 该研究为ERM树的最优性提供了原则性基础，并引入了经验过程工具，这些工具广泛适用于其他高度自适应、数据驱动的程序。PSHAB空间明确捕捉了实践中遇到的三个关键结构特征。

Abstract: While globally optimal empirical risk minimization (ERM) decision trees have become computationally feasible and empirically successful, rigorous theoretical guarantees for their statistical performance remain limited. In this work, we develop a comprehensive statistical theory for ERM trees under random design in both high-dimensional regression and classification. We first establish sharp oracle inequalities that bound the excess risk of the ERM estimator relative to the best possible approximation achievable by any tree with at most $L$ leaves, thereby characterizing the interpretability-accuracy trade-off. We derive these results using a novel uniform concentration framework based on empirically localized Rademacher complexity. Furthermore, we derive minimax optimal rates over a novel function class: the piecewise sparse heterogeneous anisotropic Besov (PSHAB) space. This space explicitly captures three key structural features encountered in practice: sparsity, anisotropic smoothness, and spatial heterogeneity. While our main results are established under sub-Gaussianity, we also provide robust guarantees that hold under heavy-tailed noise settings. Together, these findings provide a principled foundation for the optimality of ERM trees and introduce empirical process tools broadly applicable to other highly adaptive, data-driven procedures.

</details>


### [165] [Harnessing Synthetic Data from Generative AI for Statistical Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05396)
*Ahmad Abdel-Azim,Ruoyu Wang,Xihong Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文从统计学视角系统综述了生成式AI模型产生的合成数据的现状，分析了合成数据在发现、推断和预测中的有效使用条件，指出了常见陷阱，并提出了原则性使用框架和实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI模型的兴起极大地扩展了合成数据的可用性和使用范围，但这引发了关于合成数据何时能够以有效、可靠和原则性方式使用的基本统计学问题。论文旨在澄清合成数据能够有意义支持下游发现、推断和预测的假设条件。

Method: 论文采用综述方法，首先调查了现代生成模型的主要类别、预期用例及其优势，同时强调了它们的局限性和特征性失败模式。然后分析了将合成数据视为真实观测替代品时出现的常见陷阱，包括模型误设导致的偏差、不确定性衰减和泛化困难。基于这些见解，讨论了合成数据原则性使用的新兴框架。

Result: 论文系统梳理了合成数据生成和使用的现状，识别了关键挑战：模型误设偏差、不确定性衰减、泛化困难等。提出了原则性使用合成数据的框架，为方法开发者和应用研究者提供了指导。

Conclusion: 论文总结了合成数据使用的实践建议、开放问题和注意事项，旨在指导方法开发者和应用研究者。强调了在科学、工业和政策领域中负责任地使用合成数据的重要性，并指出了未来研究方向。

Abstract: The emergence of generative AI models has dramatically expanded the availability and use of synthetic data across scientific, industrial, and policy domains. While these developments open new possibilities for data analysis, they also raise fundamental statistical questions about when synthetic data can be used in a valid, reliable, and principled manner. This paper reviews the current landscape of synthetic data generation and use from a statistical perspective, with the goal of clarifying the assumptions under which synthetic data can meaningfully support downstream discovery, inference, and prediction. We survey major classes of modern generative models, their intended use cases, and the benefits they offer, while also highlighting their limitations and characteristic failure modes. We additionally examine common pitfalls that arise when synthetic data are treated as surrogates for real observations, including biases from model misspecification, attenuated uncertainty, and difficulties in generalization. Building on these insights, we discuss emerging frameworks for the principled use of synthetic data. We conclude with practical recommendations, open problems, and cautions intended to guide both method developers and applied researchers.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [SarcasmMiner: A Dual-Track Post-Training Framework for Robust Audio-Visual Sarcasm Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05275)
*Zhu Li,Yongjian Chen,Huiyuan Lai,Xiyuan Gao,Shekhar Nayak,Matt Coler*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: SarcasmMiner：基于强化学习的后训练框架，通过结构化推理和双轨蒸馏策略提升多模态讽刺检测性能，减少幻觉问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态讽刺检测需要解决文本、声学和视觉线索之间的语用不一致性，通过跨模态推理实现。现有基础模型在多模态推理中容易出现幻觉问题，需要开发能够抵抗幻觉的鲁棒推理方法。

Method: 提出SarcasmMiner框架：1）将讽刺检测重新表述为结构化推理问题；2）采用双轨蒸馏策略：高质量教师轨迹初始化学生模型，完整轨迹集训练生成式奖励模型（GenRM）评估推理质量；3）使用组相对策略优化（GRPO）通过解耦的准确性和推理质量奖励优化学生模型。

Result: 在MUStARD++数据集上，SarcasmMiner将F1分数从零样本的59.83%、监督微调的68.23%提升到70.22%。结果表明推理感知的奖励建模能同时提升性能和跨模态基础。

Conclusion: SarcasmMiner通过强化学习后训练框架有效提升了多模态讽刺检测性能，证明了结构化推理和双轨蒸馏策略在抵抗幻觉、增强跨模态推理方面的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal sarcasm detection requires resolving pragmatic incongruity across textual, acoustic, and visual cues through cross-modal reasoning. To enable robust sarcasm reasoning with foundation models, we propose SarcasmMiner, a reinforcement learning based post-training framework that resists hallucination in multimodal reasoning. We reformulate sarcasm detection as structured reasoning and adopt a dual-track distillation strategy: high-quality teacher trajectories initialize the student model, while the full set of trajectories trains a generative reward model (GenRM) to evaluate reasoning quality. The student is optimized with group relative policy optimization (GRPO) using decoupled rewards for accuracy and reasoning quality. On MUStARD++, SarcasmMiner increases F1 from 59.83% (zero-shot), 68.23% (supervised finetuning) to 70.22%. These findings suggest that reasoning-aware reward modeling enhances both performance and multimodal grounding.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Uniform convergence of kernel averages under fixed design with heterogeneous dependent data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04681)
*Danilo Hiroshi Matsuoka,Hudson da Silva Torrent*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在等间距固定设计点下核平均的一致收敛速率，适用于时间序列的非参数方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于随机设计的核平均收敛结果依赖于密度条件论证，不适用于等间距固定设计的时间序列分析。需要建立适用于确定性网格上观测的时间序列的非参数方法理论框架。

Method: 在强混合和矩条件下，利用网格结构分析核平均的弱一致和强一致收敛速率。框架允许依赖三角阵列，特别适用于确定性网格上观测的时间序列。

Result: 推导出了核平均的弱一致和强一致收敛速率，这些结果不要求平稳性。将理论应用于具有时变自回归误差的非参数回归模型中的局部线性估计器。

Conclusion: 该研究为等间距固定设计点下的核平均提供了统一收敛理论，补充了现有随机设计结果，特别适用于时间序列的非参数方法。理论结果通过蒙特卡洛实验和实证应用得到验证。

Abstract: We provide uniform convergence rates for kernel averages on $[0,1]$ under equally-spaced fixed design points of the form $x_{t,T}=t/T,\ t\in\{1,\dotsc, T\},\ T\in\mathbb{N}$. The rates of weak and strong uniform consistency are derived under strong mixing and moment conditions and do not require stationarity. The analysis exploits the grid structure and thus complements existing random-design results such as those of Hansen (2008) and Kristensen (2009), which rely on density-based conditioning arguments. The framework accommodates dependent triangular arrays and is particularly relevant for nonparametric methods applied to time series observed on deterministic grids. As an application, we derive uniform convergence rates for the local linear estimator in a nonparametric regression model with time-varying autoregressive errors. The theoretical results are illustrated through Monte Carlo experiments and an empirical application.

</details>


### [168] [Sequential Multiple Testing: A Second-Order Asymptotic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04685)
*Jingyu Liu,Yanglei Song*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文建立了序贯多重检验的二阶渐近最优性统一理论，证明了多个已知的一阶最优程序实际上是二阶最优的，并推导了最小可达期望样本量的二阶渐近展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究序贯多重检验问题，现有的一阶最优程序虽然渐近有效，但缺乏对二阶最优性的理论分析。需要建立统一的二阶渐近最优性理论，以更精确地评估检验程序的性能，并推导最小期望样本量的二阶渐近展开。

Method: 建立统一的二阶渐近最优性理论框架，提出一般充分条件证明二阶贝叶斯最优性蕴含二阶频率最优性。推导最小可达期望样本量的二阶渐近展开，识别来自多维随机游走边界穿越问题的二阶修正项。将该理论应用于广义族错误率、错误发现率和错误非发现率等常用误差度量。

Result: 证明了多个已知的一阶最优序贯检验程序实际上是二阶最优的：对于所有信号配置，当误差容忍水平趋于零时，它们的期望样本量与最小可达期望样本量之差保持有界。推导了最小可达期望样本量的二阶渐近展开，改进了经典的一阶近似。

Conclusion: 建立了序贯多重检验的二阶渐近最优性统一理论，证明了二阶贝叶斯最优性蕴含二阶频率最优性的一般条件。该理论不仅确认了多个现有程序的最优性，还提供了更精确的性能评估框架，对序贯多重检验的理论发展和实际应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study sequential multiple testing with independent data streams, where the goal is to identify an unknown subset of signals while controlling commonly used error metrics, including generalized familywise rates and false discovery and non-discovery rates. For these problems, procedures that are first-order optimal are known, in the sense that the ratio of their expected sample size (ESS) to the minimal achievable ESS converges to one as the error tolerance levels vanish. In this work, we develop a unified theory of second-order asymptotic optimality. We establish general sufficient conditions under which second-order Bayesian optimality implies second-order frequentist optimality for broad classes of sequential testing procedures. As a consequence, several procedures previously known to be first-order optimal are shown to be second-order optimal: for every signal configuration, the difference between their ESS and the minimal achievable ESS remains uniformly bounded as the error tolerance levels tend to zero. In addition, we derive a second-order asymptotic expansion of the minimal achievable ESS, which refines the classical first-order approximation by identifying the second-order correction term arising from a boundary-crossing problem for a multidimensional random walk. We apply this result to several commonly used error metrics.

</details>


### [169] [The augmented van Trees inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04686)
*Elliot H. Young*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出增强版van Trees不等式，提供比经典van Trees不等式更紧的极小极大贝叶斯风险下界，支持边界密度非零的先验分布，在非参数估计中可获得更尖锐的常数甚至精确常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典van Trees不等式在估计器极小极大风险下界推导中存在局限性：1) 提供的下界不够紧致；2) 要求先验分布在边界处密度为零；3) 在非参数估计中常数不够尖锐。需要一种更灵活、更精确的下界推导工具。

Method: 提出增强版van Trees不等式，通过数学扩展改进经典不等式，使其能够处理边界密度非零的先验分布。该方法可应用于非参数估计问题，包括高斯误差回归模型、非高斯模型、平方误差损失以外的损失函数，并可扩展到不规则模型的广义van Trees不等式。

Result: 1) 在单变量可微且导数Lipschitz的回归函数估计中，获得渐近极小化点均方误差，常数因子为1.37；2) 在高维Hölder光滑回归函数(光滑度β∈(0,2])估计中获得精确常数；3) 这些结果无法通过经典van Trees不等式获得；4) 方法适用于非高斯模型和多种损失函数。

Conclusion: 增强版van Trees不等式提供了比经典van Trees不等式更紧、更灵活的极小极大风险下界推导工具，能够处理更广泛的先验分布，在非参数估计中可获得更尖锐的常数甚至精确常数，扩展了理论统计推断的能力。

Abstract: We introduce an augmented form of the van Trees inequality, that yields uniformly tighter lower bounds on the minimax squared Bayes risk of estimators compared with the classical van Trees inequality. Our augmented inequality also accommodates prior distributions whose densities need not vanish at the boundaries of their supports. We demonstrate how this refinement can be utilised for elementary proofs of a number of minimax lower bounds for nonparametric estimands, that also often attain sharper constants than those obtained by the alternative Le Cam convergence of experiments theory and the classical van Trees inequality, and in some cases obtain exact constants. As an example, our augmented van Trees inequality can be used to obtain the asymptotic minimax pointwise mean squared error when estimating the regression function in the model with normal errors: when the regression function is univariate and differentiable with Lipschitz derivative we obtain this quantity up to a constant factor of $1.37$; and in the high dimensional regime with a Hölder smooth regression function of smoothness $β\in(0,2]$ we obtain exact constants. Both these results do not follow from an application of the classical van Trees inequality. The flexibility of our augmented van Trees inequality accommodates lower bounds for models beyond Gaussianity, loss functions beyond the squared error loss, and we are also able to incorporate this augmentation into generalised versions of the van Trees inequality for irregular models.

</details>


### [170] [Strong consistency of the local linear estimator for a generalized regression function with dependent functional data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04690)
*Danilo Hiroshi Matsuoka,Hudson da Silva Torrent*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 研究广义非参数标量对函数回归模型，针对异质分布和强混合数据，建立局部线性估计量的几乎完全收敛速率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注独立数据，实际应用中数据常存在依赖性和异质性，需要建立适用于依赖数据的非参数函数回归理论框架

Method: 采用局部线性估计方法，在异质分布和强混合数据条件下，推导回归函数的点态和一致收敛速率

Result: 证明了在紧集上点态和一致收敛速率相同；依赖数据的收敛速率慢于独立数据；模拟显示FLL优于FLC；能源消费预测中FLL精度显著更高

Conclusion: 局部线性估计在处理依赖和异质数据时具有理论优势和实际应用价值，为函数型数据的非参数回归分析提供了有效工具

Abstract: In this study, we focus on a generalized nonparametric scalar-on-function regression model for heterogeneously distributed and strongly mixing data. We provide almost complete convergence rates for the local linear estimator of the regression function. We show that, under our conditions, the pointwise and uniform convergence rates are the same on a compact set. On the other hand, when the data is dependent, it is proved that the convergence rate can be slower than those obtained for independent data. A simulation study shows the good performance and finite sample properties of the functional local linear estimator (FLL) in comparison to the local constant estimator (FLC). In addition, a one step ahead energy consumption forecasting exercise illustrates that the forecasts of the FLL estimator are significantly more accurate than those of the FLC.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [SearchGym: A Modular Infrastructure for Cross-Platform Benchmarking and Hybrid Search Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04402)
*Jerome Tze-Hou Hsu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SearchGym是一个模块化基础设施，用于跨平台基准测试和混合搜索编排，通过解耦数据表示、嵌入策略和检索逻辑实现可组合配置，在LitSearch基准上达到70%的Top-100检索率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG工具包在实验原型与生产就绪系统之间存在根本差距，需要一种能够支持跨平台基准测试和混合搜索编排的基础设施。

Method: 将数据表示、嵌入策略和检索逻辑解耦为状态抽象（Dataset、VectorSet、App），通过组合配置代数实现系统合成，并分析混合检索管道中的"Top-k认知"问题。

Result: 在LitSearch专家标注基准上达到70%的Top-100检索率，揭示了通用性与可优化性之间的设计张力。

Conclusion: SearchGym展示了工程优化可作为揭示跨异构领域信息检索中因果机制的工具，提供了开源实现。

Abstract: The rapid growth of Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has created a proliferation of toolkits, yet a fundamental gap remains between experimental prototypes and robust, production-ready systems. We present SearchGym, a modular infrastructure designed for cross-platform benchmarking and hybrid search orchestration. Unlike existing model-centric frameworks, SearchGym decouples data representation, embedding strategies, and retrieval logic into stateful abstractions: Dataset, VectorSet, and App. This separation enables a Compositional Config Algebra, allowing designers to synthesize entire systems from hierarchical configurations while ensuring perfect reproducibility. Moreover, we analyze the "Top-$k$ Cognizance" in hybrid retrieval pipelines, demonstrating that the optimal sequence of semantic ranking and structured filtering is highly dependent on filter strength. Evaluated on the LitSearch expert-annotated benchmark, SearchGym achieves a 70% Top-100 retrieval rate. SearchGym reveals a design tension between generalizability and optimizability, presenting the potential where engineering optimization may serve as a tool for uncovering the causal mechanisms inherent in information retrieval across heterogeneous domains. An open-source implementation of SearchGym is available at: https://github.com/JeromeTH/search-gym

</details>


### [172] [Signal in the Noise: Decoding the Reality of Airline Service Quality with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04404)
*Ahmed Dawoud,Osama El-Shamy,Ahmed Habashy*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 验证LLM框架从非结构化在线反馈中提取细粒度服务洞察的有效性，应用于埃及航空和阿联酋航空的TripAdvisor评论分析，揭示传统指标遗漏的关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统服务质量指标无法捕捉非结构化在线反馈中隐藏的乘客满意度驱动因素，需要更精细的分析方法来提取可操作的战略情报。

Method: 采用多阶段LLM管道分析2016-2025年间超过16,000条TripAdvisor评论，将服务问题分类为36个具体类别，识别传统指标遗漏的细粒度驱动因素。

Result: 发现埃及航空存在显著的"运营感知脱节"：尽管报告运营改进，但2022年后乘客满意度骤降（评分<2.0）。识别出传统指标遗漏的具体驱动因素，如中断期间的沟通问题和员工行为，并确定了关键旅游市场中的情感侵蚀。

Conclusion: LLM框架作为强大的诊断工具，通过将非结构化乘客声音转化为可操作的战略情报，超越了传统调查方法，为航空和旅游行业提供了有效的分析手段。

Abstract: Traditional service quality metrics often fail to capture the nuanced drivers of passenger satisfaction hidden within unstructured online feedback. This study validates a Large Language Model (LLM) framework designed to extract granular insights from such data. Analyzing over 16,000 TripAdvisor reviews for EgyptAir and Emirates (2016-2025), the study utilizes a multi-stage pipeline to categorize 36 specific service issues. The analysis uncovers a stark "operational perception disconnect" for EgyptAir: despite reported operational improvements, passenger satisfaction plummeted post-2022 (ratings < 2.0). Our approach identified specific drivers missed by conventional metrics-notably poor communication during disruptions and staff conduct-and pinpointed critical sentiment erosion in key tourism markets. These findings confirm the framework's efficacy as a powerful diagnostic tool, surpassing traditional surveys by transforming unstructured passenger voices into actionable strategic intelligence for the airline and tourism sectors.

</details>


### [173] [Still Fresh? Evaluating Temporal Drift in Retrieval Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04532)
*Nathan Kuissi,Suraj Subrahmanyan,Nandan Thakur,Jimmy Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究分析了技术检索基准FreshStack在时间推移下的语料库漂移影响，发现虽然技术文档会迁移到竞争对手仓库，但查询支持度保持稳定，检索模型排名变化很小，表明基于演化语料库的检索基准仍然可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信息检索基准遵循Cranfield范式，依赖静态预定义语料库。然而技术语料库（如API弃用、代码重组）的时间变化会导致现有基准过时。本研究旨在探究时间语料库漂移对技术领域检索基准FreshStack的影响。

Method: 研究使用FreshStack基准的两个独立语料库快照（2024年10月和2025年10月），分析LangChain相关查询。通过比较两个时间点的语料库，评估查询支持度变化和文档迁移模式。同时比较检索模型在两个快照上的准确性，使用Kendall τ相关系数等指标评估模型排名的稳定性。

Result: 分析显示：1）2024年提出的所有查询中，除一个外，在2025年语料库中仍得到完全支持，相关文档从LangChain迁移到竞争对手仓库（如LlamaIndex）；2）检索模型在两个快照上的准确性比较显示模型排名仅有微小变化，在Recall@50指标上Kendall τ相关系数高达0.978，表明强相关性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于演化时间语料库重新评估的检索基准对于检索评估仍然可靠。技术文档的迁移不会显著影响查询支持度和模型性能评估。研究公开了所有实验数据和分析工具。

Abstract: Information retrieval (IR) benchmarks typically follow the Cranfield paradigm, relying on static and predefined corpora. However, temporal changes in technical corpora, such as API deprecations and code reorganizations, can render existing benchmarks stale. In our work, we investigate how temporal corpus drift affects FreshStack, a retrieval benchmark focused on technical domains. We examine two independent corpus snapshots of FreshStack from October 2024 and October 2025 to answer questions about LangChain. Our analysis shows that all but one query posed in 2024 remain fully supported by the 2025 corpus, as relevant documents "migrate" from LangChain to competitor repositories, such as LlamaIndex. Next, we compare the accuracy of retrieval models on both snapshots and observe only minor shifts in model rankings, with overall strong correlation of up to 0.978 Kendall $τ$ at Recall@50. These results suggest that retrieval benchmarks re-judged with evolving temporal corpora can remain reliable for retrieval evaluation. We publicly release all our artifacts at https://github.com/fresh-stack/driftbench.

</details>


### [174] [Scaling Laws for Reranking in Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04816)
*Rahul Seetharaman,Aman Bansal,Hamed Zamani,Kaustubh Dhole*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 首次系统研究重排序器的缩放定律，发现性能遵循可预测的幂律规律，可通过小规模实验准确预测大规模模型性能，为工业级检索系统提供实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放定律主要关注自然语言生成和稠密检索等任务，但无法解释多阶段检索系统（特别是重排序阶段）的缩放行为。重排序作为检索系统的最终且最具影响力的步骤，其缩放规律尚未被系统研究。

Method: 通过分析模型大小和数据预算对三种主流重排序范式（点式、对式、列表式）的影响，以交叉编码器重排序器为详细案例研究，验证性能是否遵循幂律规律。使用多种损失函数、模型和指标进行实验。

Result: 重排序性能确实遵循可预测的幂律规律，NDCG和MAP等下游指标表现出可靠的缩放行为，可通过小规模实验准确预测大规模模型性能（如用400M参数模型预测1B参数模型的NDCG）。但Contrastive Entropy和MRR等指标在某些情况下不遵循可预测的缩放规律。

Conclusion: 建立了重排序的缩放原则，为构建工业级检索系统提供了可操作的见解。研究证明通过小规模实验可以准确预测大规模重排序器的性能，从而显著节省计算资源。

Abstract: Scaling laws have been observed across a wide range of tasks, such as natural language generation and dense retrieval, where performance follows predictable patterns as model size, data, and compute grow. However, these scaling laws are insufficient for understanding the scaling behavior of multi-stage retrieval systems, which typically include a reranking stage. In large-scale multi-stage retrieval systems, reranking is the final and most influential step before presenting a ranked list of items to the end user. In this work, we present the first systematic study of scaling laws for rerankers by analyzing performance across model sizes and data budgets for three popular paradigms: pointwise, pairwise, and listwise reranking. Using a detailed case study with cross-encoder rerankers, we demonstrate that performance follows a predictable power law. This regularity allows us to accurately forecast the performance of larger models for some metrics more than others using smaller-scale experiments, offering a robust methodology for saving significant computational resources. For example, we accurately estimate the NDCG of a 1B-parameter model by training and evaluating only smaller models (up to 400M parameters), in both in-domain as well as out-of-domain settings. Our experiments encompass span several loss functions, models and metrics and demonstrate that downstream metrics like NDCG, MAP (Mean Avg Precision) show reliable scaling behavior and can be forecasted accurately at scale, while highlighting the limitations of metrics like Contrastive Entropy and MRR (Mean Reciprocal Rank) which do not follow predictable scaling behavior in all instances. Our results establish scaling principles for reranking and provide actionable insights for building industrial-grade retrieval systems.

</details>


### [175] [Detecting RAG Advertisements Across Advertising Styles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04925)
*Sebastian Heineking,Wilhelm Pertsch,Ines Zelch,Janek Bevendorff,Benno Stein,Matthias Hagen,Martin Potthast*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文研究了RAG系统中LLM生成的"原生广告"检测问题，开发了广告风格分类法，评估了不同检测方法在广告风格变化下的鲁棒性，发现基于实体识别的模型效果最好但计算量大，轻量级模型则鲁棒性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在RAG系统中生成"原生广告"（有机回答与上下文相关广告混合）的兴起，需要自动检测这些广告。现有数据集未能反映营销文献中讨论的广告风格多样性，且广告商可能通过改变广告风格来规避检测。

Method: 1. 为LLM开发广告风格分类法，结合明确性和诉求类型两个维度；2. 模拟广告商通过改变广告风格来规避检测；3. 评估多种广告检测方法在这些变化下的鲁棒性，包括使用实体识别精确定位广告的模型，以及轻量级模型如随机森林和SVM。

Result: 基于实体识别的模型在检测含广告的响应方面非常有效，且对广告风格变化具有较好的鲁棒性。然而，由于广告拦截需要在低资源终端设备上运行，轻量级模型（如随机森林和SVM）在这些变化下表现脆弱，鲁棒性不足。

Conclusion: 虽然基于实体识别的模型能有效检测生成广告且鲁棒性较好，但其计算复杂度高，不适合低资源环境。轻量级模型鲁棒性不足，需要进一步开展面向效率的研究，以实现实用的生成广告拦截方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) enable a new form of advertising for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems in which organic responses are blended with contextually relevant ads. The prospect of such "generated native ads" has sparked interest in whether they can be detected automatically. Existing datasets, however, do not reflect the diversity of advertising styles discussed in the marketing literature. In this paper, we (1) develop a taxonomy of advertising styles for LLMs, combining the style dimensions of explicitness and type of appeal, (2) simulate that advertisers may attempt to evade detection by changing their advertising style, and (3) evaluate a variety of ad-detection approaches with respect to their robustness under these changes. Expanding previous work on ad detection, we train models that use entity recognition to exactly locate an ad in an LLM response and find them to be both very effective at detecting responses with ads and largely robust to changes in the advertising style. Since ad blocking will be performed on low-resource end-user devices, we include lightweight models like random forests and SVMs in our evaluation. These models, however, are brittle under such changes, highlighting the need for further efficiency-oriented research for a practical approach to blocking of generated ads.

</details>


### [176] [Debiasing Sequential Recommendation with Time-aware Inverse Propensity Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04986)
*Sirui Huang,Jing Long,Qian Li,Guandong Xu,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TIPS方法解决序列推荐中的选择偏差和曝光偏差问题，通过时间感知的反倾向评分来更准确地估计用户偏好


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐方法主要依赖显式交互（如点击、购买），忽略了物品曝光信息，导致选择偏差（将曝光未点击误解为不感兴趣）和曝光偏差（将未曝光物品视为不相关）。需要区分"未曝光"和"不感兴趣"的物品，但历史数据中的相关性无法可靠推断这种区别。

Method: 提出时间感知反倾向评分（TIPS）方法，与传统静态IPS不同，TIPS能够有效考虑序列依赖性和时间动态性，从而更准确地捕捉用户偏好。TIPS可作为各种序列推荐器的插件使用。

Result: 大量实验表明，TIPS作为插件能够持续提升各种序列推荐器的性能。

Conclusion: TIPS通过解决序列推荐中的选择偏差和曝光偏差问题，提高了推荐性能，可作为现有序列推荐方法的有效增强插件。

Abstract: Sequential Recommendation (SR) predicts users next interactions by modeling the temporal order of their historical behaviors. Existing approaches, including traditional sequential models and generative recommenders, achieve strong performance but primarily rely on explicit interactions such as clicks or purchases while overlooking item exposures. This ignorance introduces selection bias, where exposed but unclicked items are misinterpreted as disinterest, and exposure bias, where unexposed items are treated as irrelevant. Effectively addressing these biases requires distinguishing between items that were "not exposed" and those that were "not of interest", which cannot be reliably inferred from correlations in historical data. Counterfactual reasoning provides a natural solution by estimating user preferences under hypothetical exposure, and Inverse Propensity Scoring (IPS) is a common tool for such estimation. However, conventional IPS methods are static and fail to capture the sequential dependencies and temporal dynamics of user behavior. To overcome these limitations, we propose Time aware Inverse Propensity Scoring (TIPS). Unlike traditional static IPS, TIPS effectively accounts for sequential dependencies and temporal dynamics, thereby capturing user preferences more accurately. Extensive experiments show that TIPS consistently enhances recommendation performance as a plug-in for various sequential recommenders. Our code will be publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [177] [Core-based Hierarchies for Efficient GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05207)
*Jakir Hossain,Ahmet Erdem Sarıyüce*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出用k-core分解替代Leiden聚类，解决稀疏知识图中社区检测的非可重复性问题，构建确定性、密度感知的层次结构，提升GraphRAG在全局意义理解任务中的效果和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于向量的RAG方法在需要跨多文档推理的全局意义理解任务上表现不佳。GraphRAG通过知识图层次社区解决此问题，但当前基于Leiden聚类的方法在稀疏知识图上存在非可重复性问题，因为模块度优化存在指数级数量的近最优分区。

Method: 1) 用k-core分解替代Leiden聚类，获得确定性、密度感知的层次结构；2) 提出轻量级启发式方法，利用k-core层次构建大小受限、保持连通性的社区；3) 引入token预算感知的采样策略，降低LLM成本；4) 在金融财报、新闻文章、播客等真实数据集上评估，使用三个LLM生成答案和五个独立LLM法官进行头对头评估。

Result: 在多个数据集和模型上，该方法一致提高了答案的全面性和多样性，同时减少了token使用量。k-core-based GraphRAG在全局意义理解任务中表现出有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: k-core分解为GraphRAG提供了确定性、可重复的社区检测方法，解决了稀疏知识图中Leiden聚类的非可重复性问题，构建了更有效的全局意义理解框架，在保持性能的同时降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances large language models by incorporating external knowledge. However, existing vector-based methods often fail on global sensemaking tasks that require reasoning across many documents. GraphRAG addresses this by organizing documents into a knowledge graph with hierarchical communities that can be recursively summarized. Current GraphRAG approaches rely on Leiden clustering for community detection, but we prove that on sparse knowledge graphs, where average degree is constant and most nodes have low degree, modularity optimization admits exponentially many near-optimal partitions, making Leiden-based communities inherently non-reproducible. To address this, we propose replacing Leiden with k-core decomposition, which yields a deterministic, density-aware hierarchy in linear time. We introduce a set of lightweight heuristics that leverage the k-core hierarchy to construct size-bounded, connectivity-preserving communities for retrieval and summarization, along with a token-budget-aware sampling strategy that reduces LLM costs. We evaluate our methods on real-world datasets including financial earnings transcripts, news articles, and podcasts, using three LLMs for answer generation and five independent LLM judges for head-to-head evaluation. Across datasets and models, our approach consistently improves answer comprehensiveness and diversity while reducing token usage, demonstrating that k-core-based GraphRAG is an effective and efficient framework for global sensemaking.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Decorrelating the Future: Joint Frequency Domain Learning for Spatio-temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04418)
*Zepu Wang,Bowen Liao,Jeff,Ban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FreST Loss，一种频率增强的时空训练目标，通过联合傅里叶变换在统一的谱域中对齐模型预测与真实值，有效解耦时空依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直接预测模型通常依赖点状目标函数（如均方误差），无法捕捉图结构信号中复杂的时空依赖关系。虽然最近的频域方法（如FreDF）缓解了时间自相关问题，但往往忽略了空间和跨时空交互作用。

Method: 提出FreST Loss，一种频率增强的时空训练目标，通过联合傅里叶变换（JFT）将监督扩展到联合时空谱域。该方法在统一的谱域中对齐模型预测与真实值，有效解耦空间和时间上的复杂依赖关系。

Result: 理论分析表明该公式减少了与时域训练目标相关的估计偏差。在六个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验证明，FreST Loss是模型无关的，通过更好地捕捉整体时空动态，持续改进了最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: FreST Loss通过将监督扩展到联合时空谱域，有效解决了现有方法在捕捉复杂时空依赖关系方面的局限性，为图结构信号的预测提供了更全面的训练目标。

Abstract: Standard direct forecasting models typically rely on point-wise objectives such as Mean Squared Error, which fail to capture the complex spatio-temporal dependencies inherent in graph-structured signals. While recent frequency-domain approaches such as FreDF mitigate temporal autocorrelation, they often overlook spatial and cross spatio-temporal interactions. To address this limitation, we propose FreST Loss, a frequency-enhanced spatio-temporal training objective that extends supervision to the joint spatio-temporal spectrum. By leveraging the Joint Fourier Transform (JFT), FreST Loss aligns model predictions with ground truth in a unified spectral domain, effectively decorrelating complex dependencies across both space and time. Theoretical analysis shows that this formulation reduces estimation bias associated with time-domain training objectives. Extensive experiments on six real-world datasets demonstrate that FreST Loss is model-agnostic and consistently improves state-of-the-art baselines by better capturing holistic spatio-temporal dynamics.

</details>


### [179] [Delta-Crosscoder: Robust Crosscoder Model Diffing in Narrow Fine-Tuning Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04426)
*Aly Kassem,Thomas Jiralerspong,Negar Rostamzadeh,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Delta-Crosscoder：改进的模型差异分析方法，通过结合BatchTopK稀疏性、基于delta的损失和隐式对比信号，有效识别微调引起的模型内部表示变化，特别针对局部化、不对称的窄微调场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Crosscoder方法在处理窄微调（行为变化局部化且不对称）时存在困难，需要更有效的模型差异分析方法来识别微调如何改变模型的内部表示。

Method: Delta-Crosscoder结合了三种关键技术：1) BatchTopK稀疏性；2) 基于delta的损失函数，优先关注模型间变化的方向；3) 从匹配输入的配对激活中获得的隐式对比信号。该方法学习基模型和微调模型之间可解释的潜在方向共享字典。

Result: 在10个模型生物（包括合成虚假事实、涌现错位、潜意识学习和禁忌词猜测）上评估，涵盖Gemma、LLaMA、Qwen等模型（1B-9B参数）。Delta-Crosscoder可靠地分离出对微调行为有因果责任的潜在方向，并实现有效缓解，性能优于基于SAE的基线，与非SAE基线相当。

Conclusion: Delta-Crosscoder证明crosscoders仍然是模型差异分析的强大工具，能够有效处理窄微调场景，识别微调引起的模型内部表示变化。

Abstract: Model diffing methods aim to identify how fine-tuning changes a model's internal representations. Crosscoders approach this by learning shared dictionaries of interpretable latent directions between base and fine-tuned models. However, existing formulations struggle with narrow fine-tuning, where behavioral changes are localized and asymmetric. We introduce Delta-Crosscoder, which combines BatchTopK sparsity with a delta-based loss prioritizing directions that change between models, plus an implicit contrastive signal from paired activations on matched inputs. Evaluated across 10 model organisms, including synthetic false facts, emergent misalignment, subliminal learning, and taboo word guessing (Gemma, LLaMA, Qwen; 1B-9B parameters), Delta-Crosscoder reliably isolates latent directions causally responsible for fine-tuned behaviors and enables effective mitigation, outperforming SAE-based baselines, while matching the Non-SAE-based. Our results demonstrate that crosscoders remain a powerful tool for model diffing.

</details>


### [180] [Thin Keys, Full Values: Reducing KV Cache via Low-Dimensional Attention Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04427)
*Hengshuai Yao,Guan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出不对称注意力机制，打破查询、键、值维度对称的传统，证明选择操作（查询和键）比值传递需要更低的维度，可实现显著的内存节省和性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer注意力机制中查询、键、值使用相同维度，但作者认为这些组件具有根本不同的功能：查询和键负责产生标量注意力权重（选择），而值携带丰富的语义表示（值传递）。选择本质上比值传递需要更低的维度，仅需O(log N)维度即可区分N个相关模式。

Method: 1. 理论分析：论证选择操作是低维操作，仅需O(log N)维度；2. 实验验证：通过七个实验验证假设，包括位置选择任务、内容检索、语言建模（WikiText-2/103）、GPT-2的SVD后训练压缩、125M参数LLaMA模型验证、Mistral-7B模型应用；3. 提出SVD压缩+QK微调方法：对现有模型进行奇异值分解压缩，然后对查询和键进行轻量级微调恢复质量损失。

Result: 1. 位置选择任务仅需每头1个维度；2. 内容检索需要约log₂N维度；3. WikiText语言建模中，d_select = d_model/4仅增加4.3%困惑度，同时减少75%QK参数；4. GPT-2的SVD压缩显示键比查询更容易压缩；5. 125M参数LLaMA模型验证了相同的退化比率；6. Mistral-7B模型通过SVD压缩+QK微调实现75%键缓存节省，仅2.0%质量损失；7. 对于7B参数模型服务128K上下文，不对称注意力每用户节省25GB KV缓存，使相同GPU上并发用户增加约60%。

Conclusion: 不对称注意力机制打破了传统Transformer中查询、键、值维度对称的限制，证明选择操作（查询和键）比值传递需要更低的维度。通过SVD压缩和轻量级QK微调，可以在几乎不损失质量的情况下显著减少键缓存内存占用，提高模型服务效率，为大规模语言模型部署提供了实用的内存优化方案。

Abstract: Standard transformer attention uses identical dimensionality for queries, keys, and values ($d_q = d_k = d_v = \dmodel$). Our insight is that these components serve fundamentally different roles, and this symmetry is unnecessary. Queries and keys produce scalar attention weights (\emph{selection}), while values carry rich semantic representations (\emph{value transfer}). We argue that selection is an inherently lower-dimensional operation than value transfer, requiring only $\BigO(\log N)$ dimensions to distinguish among $N$ relevant patterns. We validate this hypothesis across seven experiments: (1)~positional selection tasks requiring just 1~dimension per head, (2)~content-based retrieval requiring $\sim\!\log_2 N$ dimensions, (3--4)~WikiText-2 and WikiText-103 language modeling where $\dselect = \dmodel/4$ incurs only 4.3\% perplexity increase while reducing QK parameters by 75\%, (5)~post-training SVD compression of GPT-2, revealing keys to be far more compressible than queries, with lightweight QK fine-tuning recovering nearly all quality loss, (6)~a 125M-parameter LLaMA model confirming identical degradation ratios across architectures, and (7)~Mistral-7B (7.2B parameters), where SVD compression followed by QK fine-tuning achieves 75\% key cache savings at just 2.0\% residual quality cost. For existing models, SVD compression followed by QK fine-tuning (3 epochs on a small fraction of pretraining data) achieves 75\% key cache savings at $<$2\% residual quality cost. For a 7B-parameter model serving 128K context, asymmetric attention saves 25\,GB of KV cache per user, enabling approximately 60\% more concurrent users on the same GPU.

</details>


### [181] [Agent Memory Below the Prompt: Persistent Q4 KV Cache for Multi-Agent LLM Inference on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04428)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种在边缘设备上持久化存储多智能体LLM系统KV缓存的方法，通过4位量化将缓存保存到磁盘，消除重复的预填充计算，显著减少首token生成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备RAM有限，无法同时容纳多个智能体的KV缓存，导致频繁的缓存驱逐和重新预填充，造成严重的性能开销（每个智能体在4K上下文下需要15.7秒重新预填充）。

Method: 采用三个核心组件：1）块池提供每个智能体隔离的Q4 KV缓存（safetensors格式）；2）BatchQuantizedKVCache支持多智能体量化缓存的并发推理；3）跨阶段上下文注入，无需重新计算即可累积注意力状态。

Result: 缓存恢复将首token生成时间减少高达136倍（Gemma：4K-32K下22-136倍；DeepSeek：4K-32K下11-76倍；Llama：4K-16K下24-111倍）。Q4量化相比FP16在固定设备内存中容纳4倍多的智能体上下文，困惑度影响较小（Gemma -0.7%，Llama +2.8%，DeepSeek +3.0%）。

Conclusion: 通过持久化量化KV缓存的方法，有效解决了边缘设备上多智能体LLM系统的内存管理问题，显著提升了推理效率，同时保持可接受的精度损失。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems on edge devices face a memory management problem: device RAM is too small to hold every agent's KV cache simultaneously. On Apple M4 Pro with 10.2 GB of cache budget, only 3 agents fit at 8K context in FP16. A 10-agent workflow must constantly evict and reload caches. Without persistence, every eviction forces a full re-prefill through the model -- 15.7 seconds per agent at 4K context. We address this by persisting each agent's KV cache to disk in 4-bit quantized format and reloading it directly into the attention layer, eliminating redundant O(n) prefill computation via direct cache restoration. The system comprises three components: a block pool providing per-agent isolated Q4 KV caches in safetensors format, a BatchQuantizedKVCache for concurrent inference over multiple agents' quantized caches, and cross-phase context injection that accumulates attention state across conversation phases without re-computation. Evaluated on three architectures (Gemma 3 12B, dense GQA, 48 layers; DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite 16B, MoE MLA, 27 layers; Llama 3.1 8B, dense GQA, 32 layers), cache restoration reduces time-to-first-token by up to 136x (Gemma: 22--136x at 4K--32K; DeepSeek: 11--76x at 4K--32K; Llama: 24--111x at 4K--16K; 3--10x at 1K). Q4 quantization fits 4x more agent contexts into fixed device memory than FP16. Perplexity measured with actual Q4 KV caches shows -0.7% for Gemma, +2.8% for Llama, and +3.0% for DeepSeek. Open-source at https://github.com/yshk-mxim/agent-memory

</details>


### [182] [Uncertainty-Calibrated Spatiotemporal Field Diffusion with Sparse Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04431)
*Kevin Valencia,Xihaier Luo,Shinjae Yoo,David Keetae Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOLID：一种仅使用稀疏观测数据学习时空动力学的掩码条件扩散框架，无需密集场数据或预插值，通过严格稀疏条件路径实现与测量一致的全场后验采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理场通常只在稀疏、时变的传感器位置观测到，这使得预测和重建成为不适定且不确定性关键的问题。现有方法通常在密集再分析或模拟数据上训练，仅在稀疏条件下测试，而SOLID旨在直接从稀疏观测中学习时空动力学。

Method: 提出SOLID掩码条件扩散框架：1) 仅在观测目标位置进行训练和评估，无需密集场和预插值；2) 每个去噪步骤以测量值及其位置为条件；3) 引入双重掩码目标：强调在未观测空白区域学习，同时增加输入和目标提供最可靠锚点的重叠像素权重；4) 严格的稀疏条件路径实现与测量一致的全场后验采样。

Result: 在严重稀疏条件下，SOLID实现了高达一个数量级的概率误差改进，并产生校准的不确定性地图（ρ > 0.7），显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SOLID框架成功地从稀疏观测中学习时空动力学，无需密集场数据，通过严格的稀疏条件路径实现了准确的全场重建和校准的不确定性量化，为物理场观测的稀疏数据问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Physical fields are typically observed only at sparse, time-varying sensor locations, making forecasting and reconstruction ill-posed and uncertainty-critical. We present SOLID, a mask-conditioned diffusion framework that learns spatiotemporal dynamics from sparse observations alone: training and evaluation use only observed target locations, requiring no dense fields and no pre-imputation. Unlike prior work that trains on dense reanalysis or simulations and only tests under sparsity, SOLID is trained end-to-end with sparse supervision only. SOLID conditions each denoising step on the measured values and their locations, and introduces a dual-masking objective that (i) emphasizes learning in unobserved void regions while (ii) upweights overlap pixels where inputs and targets provide the most reliable anchors. This strict sparse-conditioning pathway enables posterior sampling of full fields consistent with the measurements, achieving up to an order-of-magnitude improvement in probabilistic error and yielding calibrated uncertainty maps (\r{ho} > 0.7) under severe sparsity.

</details>


### [183] [ZorBA: Zeroth-order Federated Fine-tuning of LLMs with Heterogeneous Block Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04436)
*Chuiyang Meng,Ming Tang,Vincent W. S. Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZorBA：基于零阶优化的联邦微调框架，通过异构块激活机制减少VRAM使用和通信开销


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦微调大型语言模型面临两个主要挑战：1) 本地更新需要大量VRAM存储梯度；2) 频繁模型交换导致高通信开销。需要一种既能减少内存占用又能降低通信成本的高效联邦微调方法。

Method: 提出ZorBA框架，包含三个关键技术：1) 零阶优化消除客户端梯度存储；2) 异构块激活机制，服务器为不同客户端分配不同的Transformer块子集；3) 共享随机种子和梯度有限差分减少通信开销。通过理论分析块激活决策对收敛速度和VRAM使用的影响，并设计ε约束字典序算法优化块激活决策。

Result: 实验结果表明，ZorBA在VRAM使用上比三个联邦微调基线方法最多减少62.41%，同时保持较低的通信开销，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: ZorBA通过结合零阶优化和异构块激活机制，有效解决了联邦微调LLMs中的VRAM使用和通信开销问题，为分布式大模型微调提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) enables collaborative tuning across distributed clients. However, due to the large size of LLMs, local updates in federated learning (FL) may incur substantial video random-access memory (VRAM) usage. Moreover, frequent model exchange may lead to significant communication overhead. To tackle these challenges, in this paper we propose ZorBA, a zeroth-order optimization-based federated fine-tuning framework with heterogeneous block activation. ZorBA leverages zeroth-order optimization to eliminate the storage of gradients at the clients by forward passes. ZorBA includes a heterogeneous block activation mechanism in which the central server allocates different subsets of transformer blocks to clients in order to accelerate the convergence rate and reduce the VRAM usage. Furthermore, ZorBA utilizes shared random seeds and the finite differences of gradients in order to reduce the communication overhead. We conduct theoretical analysis to characterize the effect of block activation decisions on the convergence rate and VRAM usage. To jointly enhance the convergence rate and reduce the VRAM usage, we formulate an optimization problem to optimize the block activation decisions. We propose an $ε$-constraint lexicographic algorithm to solve this problem. Experimental results show that ZorBA outperforms three federated fine-tuning baselines in VRAM usage by up to 62.41% and incurs a low communication overhead.

</details>


### [184] [Learning Unified Distance Metric for Heterogeneous Attribute Data Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04458)
*Yiqun Zhang,Mingjie Zhao,Yizhou Chen,Yang Lu,Yiu-ming Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HARR学习范式，通过将异构数值和分类属性投影到统一可学习的多空间中，实现混合数据的聚类分析，无需参数且收敛有保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实聚类任务中混合数据（数值和分类属性）普遍存在，但两者本质不同：数值属性在欧氏空间有明确距离，分类属性则是隐含空间中的不同概念。现有方法要么将异构属性编码为单一类型，要么定义统一度量，未能揭示其内在联系。

Method: 提出异构属性重构与表示（HARR）学习范式：1）将异构属性转换为同构状态进行距离度量学习；2）将度量学习与聚类集成，自动适应不同聚类任务；3）将每个属性值投影到统一可学习的多空间中，精细表示和学习分类数据的距离度量。

Result: HARR方法无需参数、收敛有保证，能更有效地自适应不同聚类数量k。大量实验证明其在准确性和效率方面的优越性。

Conclusion: 通过研究任意类型属性间的联系，提出的HARR范式能有效处理混合数据聚类问题，相比现有方法在揭示属性内在联系和自适应聚类任务方面表现更优。

Abstract: Datasets composed of numerical and categorical attributes (also called mixed data hereinafter) are common in real clustering tasks. Differing from numerical attributes that indicate tendencies between two concepts (e.g., high and low temperature) with their values in well-defined Euclidean distance space, categorical attribute values are different concepts (e.g., different occupations) embedded in an implicit space. Simultaneously exploiting these two very different types of information is an unavoidable but challenging problem, and most advanced attempts either encode the heterogeneous numerical and categorical attributes into one type, or define a unified metric for them for mixed data clustering, leaving their inherent connection unrevealed. This paper, therefore, studies the connection among any-type of attributes and proposes a novel Heterogeneous Attribute Reconstruction and Representation (HARR) learning paradigm accordingly for cluster analysis. The paradigm transforms heterogeneous attributes into a homogeneous status for distance metric learning, and integrates the learning with clustering to automatically adapt the metric to different clustering tasks. Differing from most existing works that directly adopt defined distance metrics or learn attribute weights to search clusters in a subspace. We propose to project the values of each attribute into unified learnable multiple spaces to more finely represent and learn the distance metric for categorical data. HARR is parameter-free, convergence-guaranteed, and can more effectively self-adapt to different sought number of clusters $k$. Extensive experiments illustrate its superiority in terms of accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [185] [VSPrefill: Vertical-Slash Sparse Attention with Lightweight Indexing for Long-Context Prefilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04460)
*Chen Guanzhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VSPrefill：一种基于垂直-斜线结构模式的稀疏注意力机制，通过轻量级训练实现长上下文推理加速，在保持98.35%准确率的同时获得4.95倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力在预填充阶段的二次复杂度阻碍了大语言模型的长上下文推理。现有稀疏注意力方法需要在上下文适应性、采样开销和微调成本之间进行权衡。

Method: 提出VSPrefill机制，利用注意力分布中的垂直-斜线结构模式。通过紧凑的VSIndexer模块从增强RoPE的键值表示中预测上下文感知的重要性分数，为垂直列和斜线对角线构建稀疏掩码。采用自适应累积阈值策略分配每层稀疏预算，并使用融合内核执行带实时索引合并的注意力计算。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Instruct和LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct模型上，使用LongBench和RULER基准测试，VSPrefill在128k上下文长度下保持了98.35%的全注意力准确率，同时实现了4.95倍的平均加速。

Conclusion: VSPrefill在准确性和效率之间的权衡中建立了新的帕累托前沿，为长上下文推理提供了一种有效的稀疏注意力解决方案。

Abstract: The quadratic complexity of self-attention during the prefill phase impedes long-context inference in large language models. Existing sparse attention methods face a trade-off among context adaptivity, sampling overhead, and fine-tuning costs. We propose VSPrefill, a mechanism requiring lightweight training that uses the vertical-slash structural pattern in attention distributions. Our compact VSIndexer module predicts context-aware importance scores for vertical columns and slash diagonals from key-value representations augmented with RoPE. This approach constructs sparse masks with linear complexity without modifying the backbone parameters. During inference, an adaptive cumulative-threshold strategy allocates sparsity budgets per layer, while a fused kernel executes attention with on-the-fly index merging. Evaluated on Qwen3-4B-Instruct and LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct across the LongBench and RULER benchmarks, VSPrefill preserves 98.35% of the full attention accuracy while delivering a 4.95x average speedup at a context length of 128k. These results establish a new Pareto frontier in the trade-off between accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [186] [MAD-SmaAt-GNet: A Multimodal Advection-Guided Neural Network for Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04461)
*Samuel van Wonderen,Siamak Mehrkanoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAD-SmaAt-GNet扩展了SmaAt-UNet，通过多模态编码器和物理平流组件改进降水临近预报，在4小时预报中MSE降低8.9%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值预报方法计算成本高且未能充分利用大量天气数据，深度学习模型在降水临近预报中显示出潜力，但现有CNN模型如SmaAt-UNet仍有改进空间

Method: 提出MAD-SmaAt-GNet，在SmaAt-UNet基础上：(1)增加额外编码器处理多模态天气变量输入；(2)集成基于物理的平流组件确保预测的物理一致性

Result: 每个扩展单独都能改进降雨预报，组合使用效果更佳；相比基线SmaAt-UNet，4步降水预报（最长4小时）的MSE降低8.9%；多模态输入对短期预报特别有益，平流组件在长短预报时域均提升性能

Conclusion: MAD-SmaAt-GNet通过结合多模态学习和物理约束，显著提升了降水临近预报的准确性和物理一致性，为深度学习在气象预报中的应用提供了有效框架

Abstract: Precipitation nowcasting (short-term forecasting) is still often performed using numerical solvers for physical equations, which are computationally expensive and make limited use of the large volumes of available weather data. Deep learning models have shown strong potential for precipitation nowcasting, offering both accuracy and computational efficiency. Among these models, convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are particularly effective for image-to-image prediction tasks. The SmaAt-UNet is a lightweight CNN based architecture that has demonstrated strong performance for precipitation nowcasting. This paper introduces the Multimodal Advection-Guided Small Attention GNet (MAD-SmaAt-GNet), which extends the core SmaAt-UNet by (i) incorporating an additional encoder to learn from multiple weather variables and (ii) integrating a physics-based advection component to ensure physically consistent predictions. We show that each extension individually improves rainfall forecasts and that their combination yields further gains. MAD-SmaAt-GNet reduces the mean squared error (MSE) by 8.9% compared with the baseline SmaAt-UNet for four-step precipitation forecasting up to four hours ahead. Additionally, experiments indicate that multimodal inputs are particularly beneficial for short lead times, while the advection-based component enhances performance across both short and long forecasting horizons.

</details>


### [187] [Oracle-efficient Hybrid Learning with Constrained Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04546)
*Princewill Okoroafor,Robert Kleinberg,Michael P. Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种在混合在线学习问题中同时实现统计最优性和计算效率的新算法，通过约束对手从固定函数类中选择标签，利用ERM oracle高效运行，并获得与假设类和对手标签类的Rademacher复杂度相关的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合在线学习问题中，特征从未知分布中i.i.d.抽取而标签由对手生成，现有研究存在统计最优但计算不可行与计算高效但统计次优的二分困境。本文旨在同时实现统计最优性和计算效率。

Method: 考虑结构化设置，约束对手从表达力强但固定的函数类R中选择标签。提出新学习算法，利用ERM oracle高效运行，采用带有"截断熵正则化器"的Frank-Wolfe约简和新的"混合"鞅差序列尾界等技术工具。

Result: 算法获得与从学习者假设类H和对手标签类R导出的类的Rademacher复杂度相关的遗憾界。作为关键推论，为具有低维结构的高维动作集随机零和博弈提供了oracle高效的均衡计算算法。

Conclusion: 本文在混合学习设置中向同时实现统计最优性和计算效率迈出了重要一步，通过结构化约束和新技术工具开发，解决了现有二分困境，并为随机博弈提供了实用算法。

Abstract: The Hybrid Online Learning Problem, where features are drawn i.i.d. from an unknown distribution but labels are generated adversarially, is a well-motivated setting positioned between statistical and fully-adversarial online learning. Prior work has presented a dichotomy: algorithms that are statistically-optimal, but computationally intractable (Wu et al., 2023), and algorithms that are computationally-efficient (given an ERM oracle), but statistically-suboptimal (Wu et al., 2024).
  This paper takes a significant step towards achieving statistical optimality and computational efficiency simultaneously in the Hybrid Learning setting. To do so, we consider a structured setting, where the Adversary is constrained to pick labels from an expressive, but fixed, class of functions $R$. Our main result is a new learning algorithm, which runs efficiently given an ERM oracle and obtains regret scaling with the Rademacher complexity of a class derived from the Learner's hypothesis class $H$ and the Adversary's label class $R$. As a key corollary, we give an oracle-efficient algorithm for computing equilibria in stochastic zero-sum games when action sets may be high-dimensional but the payoff function exhibits a type of low-dimensional structure. Technically, we develop a number of tools for the design and analysis of our learning algorithm, including a novel Frank-Wolfe reduction with "truncated entropy regularizer" and a new tail bound for sums of "hybrid" martingale difference sequences.

</details>


### [188] [Understanding the Dynamics of Demonstration Conflict in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04464)
*Difan Jiao,Di Wang,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大型语言模型在上下文学习中处理冲突演示的能力，发现模型对包含错误规则的单个演示表现出系统性误导行为，揭示了模型内部处理冲突证据的两阶段计算结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文学习使大语言模型能够通过少量演示执行新任务，但演示本身可能包含噪声和冲突示例，使这种能力变得脆弱。研究者旨在理解模型如何处理此类冲突，特别是需要模型推断底层模式的演示依赖任务。

Method: 研究采用线性探针和logit lens分析技术，探究模型在冲突演示下的内部处理机制。通过识别注意力头在推理失败中的作用，并采用针对性消融实验验证发现。

Result: 研究发现：1）模型在单个包含错误规则的演示下性能显著下降；2）在中间层同时编码正确和错误规则，但仅在后期层形成预测置信度；3）识别出两个关键注意力头类型：早期到中间层的"易受攻击头"和后期层的"易受影响头"；4）屏蔽少量识别出的注意力头可将性能提升超过10%。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了大型语言模型在上下文学习中处理冲突证据的两阶段计算结构，为理解模型推理脆弱性提供了机制性解释，并为改进模型鲁棒性提供了具体方向。

Abstract: In-context learning enables large language models to perform novel tasks through few-shot demonstrations. However, demonstrations per se can naturally contain noise and conflicting examples, making this capability vulnerable. To understand how models process such conflicts, we study demonstration-dependent tasks requiring models to infer underlying patterns, a process we characterize as rule inference. We find that models suffer substantial performance degradation from a single demonstration with corrupted rule. This systematic misleading behavior motivates our investigation of how models process conflicting evidence internally. Using linear probes and logit lens analysis, we discover that under corruption models encode both correct and incorrect rules in intermediate layers but develop prediction confidence only in late layers, revealing a two-phase computational structure. We then identify attention heads for each phase underlying the reasoning failures: Vulnerability Heads in early-to-middle layers exhibit positional attention bias with high sensitivity to corruption, while Susceptible Heads in late layers significantly reduce support for correct predictions when exposed to the corrupted evidence. Targeted ablation validates our findings, with masking a small number of identified heads improving performance by over 10%.

</details>


### [189] [Towards Explainable Deep Learning for Ship Trajectory Prediction in Inland Waterways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04472)
*Tom Legel,Dirk Söffker,Roland Schätzle,Kathrin Donandt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于LSTM的船舶轨迹预测模型，通过融入训练得到的船舶领域参数来增强模型的可解释性，特别针对内河航道复杂交通场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内河航道拥挤环境下的船舶轨迹预测对航行安全至关重要。虽然深度学习模型在预测精度上有所提升，但这些模型往往缺乏可解释性，可能掩盖不准确的逻辑并削弱对其可靠性的信任。

Method: 采用基于LSTM的船舶轨迹预测模型，融入训练得到的船舶领域参数，通过基于注意力的机制融合交互船舶的隐藏状态。模型变体使用标准位移误差统计进行评估，并分析生成的船舶领域值的合理性。

Result: 在5分钟预测范围内，最终位移误差约为40米，与类似研究性能相当。船舶间注意力架构提升了预测精度，但使用学习到的船舶领域值分配的船舶权重与预期存在偏差，表明精度提升不完全由预测轨迹与附近船舶轨迹之间的因果关系驱动。

Conclusion: 该模型通过其内在可解释的设计展示了良好的解释能力。未来工作将集中于利用该架构进行反事实分析，并融入更复杂的注意力机制。

Abstract: Accurate predictions of ship trajectories in crowded environments are essential to ensure safety in inland waterways traffic. Recent advances in deep learning promise increased accuracy even for complex scenarios. While the challenge of ship-to-ship awareness is being addressed with growing success, the explainability of these models is often overlooked, potentially obscuring an inaccurate logic and undermining the confidence in their reliability. This study examines an LSTM-based vessel trajectory prediction model by incorporating trained ship domain parameters that provide insight into the attention-based fusion of the interacting vessels' hidden states. This approach has previously been explored in the field of maritime shipping, yet the variety and complexity of encounters in inland waterways allow for a more profound analysis of the model's interpretability. The prediction performance of the proposed model variants are evaluated using standard displacement error statistics. Additionally, the plausibility of the generated ship domain values is analyzed. With an final displacement error of around 40 meters in a 5-minute prediction horizon, the model performs comparably to similar studies. Though the ship-to-ship attention architecture enhances prediction accuracy, the weights assigned to vessels in encounters using the learnt ship domain values deviate from the expectation. The observed accuracy improvements are thus not entirely driven by a causal relationship between a predicted trajectory and the trajectories of nearby ships. This finding underscores the model's explanatory capabilities through its intrinsically interpretable design. Future work will focus on utilizing the architecture for counterfactual analysis and on the incorporation of more sophisticated attention mechanisms.

</details>


### [190] [Distributional Equivalence in Linear Non-Gaussian Latent-Variable Cyclic Causal Models: Characterization and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04780)
*Haoyue Dai,Immanuel Albrecht,Peter Spirtes,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文建立了线性非高斯模型中具有任意潜在结构和循环的因果图的等价性准则，填补了无结构假设下潜在变量因果发现的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在变量因果发现方法依赖强结构假设（如特定指标模式或交互限制），缺乏一般性、无结构假设的方法。核心障碍是缺乏等价性表征：不知道什么可识别，就无法设计识别方法。

Method: 提出边缘秩约束作为新工具，建立任意潜在结构和循环图的分布等价性图形准则。提供遍历整个等价类的过程，并开发从数据恢复模型（直至等价性）的算法。

Result: 首次在无结构假设的参数化设置中建立了潜在变量等价性表征，开发了首个无结构假设的发现方法。代码和交互演示已公开。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了线性非高斯模型中潜在变量因果发现的等价性表征空白，为无结构假设的因果发现提供了理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: Causal discovery with latent variables is a fundamental task. Yet most existing methods rely on strong structural assumptions, such as enforcing specific indicator patterns for latents or restricting how they can interact with others. We argue that a core obstacle to a general, structural-assumption-free approach is the lack of an equivalence characterization: without knowing what can be identified, one generally cannot design methods for how to identify it. In this work, we aim to close this gap for linear non-Gaussian models. We establish the graphical criterion for when two graphs with arbitrary latent structure and cycles are distributionally equivalent, that is, they induce the same observed distribution set. Key to our approach is a new tool, edge rank constraints, which fills a missing piece in the toolbox for latent-variable causal discovery in even broader settings. We further provide a procedure to traverse the whole equivalence class and develop an algorithm to recover models from data up to such equivalence. To our knowledge, this is the first equivalence characterization with latent variables in any parametric setting without structural assumptions, and hence the first structural-assumption-free discovery method. Code and an interactive demo are available at https://equiv.cc.

</details>


### [191] [Non-Euclidean Gradient Descent Operates at the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05002)
